## Calcutta

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. We were sleeping in the greatest of illusions. We blindly followed the blind and were rushing down into the abyss of *tamo-guna.* We foolishly thought happiness was gratifying the senses and were entangled in the most useless and sinful activities. Never for a moment did we consider what the actual substance was. Now your wonderful movement has most mercifully come to pick us up from Māyā. You have convinced us that Kṛṣṇa is the Supreme Personality of Godhead, that we are His eternal loving servants, and real happiness can only be obtained by serving Kṛṣṇa. But we are most unfortunate and frivolous children who do not see the gravity of spiritual life. We easily let ourselves be diverted from following your vital instructions. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray to you that we may endeavor to surrender unto you and keep consciousness focused at your lotus feet. Please let us be enthusiastic to give your divine association to other fellow youths. How can we desire to study your phenomenal books regularly and to relish the Bhaktivedanta purports? Please protect us form committing offenses to Vaiṣṇava. And we pray to you that we may remain humbly serving your faithful servants.

*Aspiring to be your granddisciples,*  
*Your unworthy servants,*  
Staff of Nava-yauvanam

## Cape Town

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

All glories to Your Divine Grace. Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your feet.

Five hundred years ago Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu began His *saṅkīrtana* movement of the chanting of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa. Recently the *saṅkīrtana* movement has been continued by you. To spread the *saṅkīrtana* movement means literally to do the work of God, because God also comes personally to establish the chanting of His names.

Caitanya Mahāprabhu stated that to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness one must be empowered by Kṛṣṇa. In this way we know that you had the full blessings of Kṛṣṇa upon you. In this way we know that you had the full blessings of Kṛṣṇa upon you. The success in preaching which you accomplished it was always simple and straightforward. You never resorted to displays of mystic power or magic to fool the mass of people. Nor did you create a very grand image of yourself. It is a fact that you made “Hare Kṛṣṇa” a household word around the world, and yet not many people know about you, the one who caused this to happen.

You simply tried to fulfill the order of your spiritual master in a humble way, according to circumstance. You never concocted any method of approaching God. You presented authorized books upon Kṛṣṇa, and you performed *kirtana* in public.

These activities are so simple and pure that they can be performed by anyone. You took the initiative and presented these things to the world. Now we can follow in your footsteps and also perform these simple activities. You have broken down all barriers and made this Kṛṣṇa consciousness available to everyone. Even the most simple, uneducated person or child can participate.

It is true that the Supreme Lord Kṛṣṇa wants us all to return to Him, and therefore He comes Himself or sends His representative. At the same time it is also true that the representative of the Lord has his own desire to see us all benedicted by Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Out of his compassion he enters into our midst. It is your mercy to us, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you kindly came to give us Kṛṣṇa. Although we are not intimately connected with Kṛṣṇa as you are, and we are generally unqualified, still we can also try in a simple, humble way to give others Kṛṣṇa. Actually, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you made our task simple, because you gave us everything. We only have to give others what you have given us.

*Your servants in Cape Town,*

Vardhanya dāsa / Bhakta Cliff / Bhakta Julian / Bhakta Daniel / Bhakta David / Bhakta Patrick / Rādhā-vinoda-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Lesley / Bhaktin Samantha

## Chandigarh

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Most respected and honorable Śrīla Prabhupāda,

Kindly accept our humble obeisances unto your divine lotus feet, and empower us to say a few words on this most auspicious and pious day which has changed the courses of our lives. Although we look foolish in our attempt to praise Your Divine Grace—as you cannot be adequately glorified even by millions of Śeṣa Nāgas, each having a thousand mouths—yet, prompted by your everlasting love and affection for us, we dare murmur a few sentences as our offering on your Vyāsa-pūjā.

Like Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu, you appeared on the dark horizon of the world—this day ninety-two years ago—like a full moon radiating bright and cool transcendental light on all humanity, thus removing the darkness of ignorance. You made the path of devotional service very smooth, trouble free, and straight for one and all, by personally removing the thorns and pebbles of voidism and impersonalism. To take complete care of the boarding and lodging and comforts of the travelers, you planted shady trees laden with nectarean juicy fruits all along the devotional path in the form of temples. All one requires to reach back home, back to Godhead, is just a little desire; the rest has been arranged by Your Divine Grace. Your compassion, your concern for the real welfare of the fallen souls of Kali-yuga, has been unparalleled in the annals of human history. Leaving the most cherished land of Vṛndāvana at an advanced age, what transcendentalist would even think of sailing for America—the kingdom of Kali-yuga—to preach the message of Lord Caitanya to the people who had never heard of Him? It could only be you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Faithfully following the instructions of your *Guru* Mahārāja, you ventured this impossible task without any financial or institutional support and came out with spectacular success.

“What was so special about you?” people amazed at your miraculous achievements ask. Well, you were all special—specialty personified. You were a perfect disciple; a perfect spiritual master; a scholar par-excellence; an affectionate and loving father to your disciples; an expert manager, cook, teacher and musician; a prolific writer. We cannot even count your innumerable qualities, what to think of describing them. You were a perfect devotee of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa (the word *perfect* getting its definition from you.) However, the unique quality that made you the moon amongst countless starlike *sādhus* and *mahātmās* in the world was your proper emphasis on the practical side of religion. A lot of saints dwell very impressively on spiritual philosophy in their discourses, and the audience is fully engrossed and spellbound. But after the *pravacana* is over, the listeners cannot decide how to adopt the sermon in practical life. What to do at home? One cannot live on philosophy alone. This was not the case with you. Śrīla Prabhupāda! While you expounded the intricacies, you took special care to tell the audience how to follow the scriptural injunctions, in their day-to-day life; chant the *mahā-mantra,* follow the four regulative principles, eat *Kṛṣṇa-prasādam,* and associate with the devotees for the purpose of hearing *Kṛṣṇa-kathā.* So simple. Anybody and everybody—educated or uneducated ; man or woman; child or grown up; black or white; Hindu, Muslim or Christian; American or Russian; *brahmana* or *śūdra*—can do it, and can do it without any change of position or status. Naturally, when we say that the human birth is very precious and is meant only for self-realization, the process of self-realization has to be such that everyone can follow it. Your Divine Grace realized this need, and you gave practical Kṛṣṇa consciousness philosophy to mankind—not only the philosophy; you also provided all the guidance and facilities for a sincere soul to cultivate Kṛṣṇa consciousness and achieve the highest goal of life. This is your specialization, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Your Divine Grace practically showed that every material thing or propensity can be spiritualized by using it in the service of Kṛṣṇa. Being fully absorbed in Kṛṣṇa consciousness (Kṛṣṇa-māyā), you Kṛṣṇa-ized everything that you came in contact with. You are an immortal touchstone, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You have invested your full power in your instructions. Anyone can touch you through your instructions and become spiritualized.

Can there be a better benefactor of humanity than yourself? With heartfelt gratitude, our heads bow down in reverence at your lotus feet today, and we feel spiritually charged while remembering your pastimes, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Kindly give us the blessings so that we may never forget your instructions and always remain in sound Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

*Your loving children,*  
Rādhā-ramaṇa dāsa / Rādhā-vallabha dāsa / Kṛṣṇa-kānti dāsa / Nanda Mahārāja dāsa and other devotees of Chandigarh Temple.

## Chicago

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our obeisances at your lotus feet. Although not so humble, we pray to become your obedient and submissive servants.

We are undeserving, but by the Lord’s causeless mercy we have come to your association, and by the effect of both your words and Kṛṣṇa’s instructions, our appreciation for you and eagerness to serve you is increasing. Please let us be sincere to know you correctly.

You have given us the real substance, which alone can replace our material attachments and purify our material desires. You have given us Kṛṣṇa in the form of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* and *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is.* You have brought the holy name and taught us the process of associating with the Lord by hearing and chanting.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our *ācārya,* our *Mahājana,* who can settle all differences of opinion, and within whose heart the real religious principles of this age are preserved. But in the mood of Lord Caitanya, the most confidential and secret things are pouring from your heart in the purports of your books. The sweet fruits of Kṛṣṇa consciousness are being freely given for the whole world to taste and to become happy.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you desire that (and the whole world needs it) there be first-class men who can preach Kṛṣṇa’s message and convince others to hear you. When will we actually become your real disciples—men of ideal character? When will we be as you are—steady in the blissful Kṛṣṇa consciousness (*kṣānti),* utilizing every moment in the loving service of the Lord (*avyartha-kālatvam)*, completely eager for constantly glorifying the Lord (*virakti),* and prideless (*māna-śūnyatā)?*

Hare Kṛṣṇa! We pray to the holy name and hope against hope that we may attain that transcendental goal of pure devotional service at your lotus feet, by which we may gain the full mercy of the Lord and become His qualified servants.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our Vyāsadeva. We know we can serve you properly if we can just hear you. As you are a direct representative of Lord Caitanya, we also know your desire is that Kṛṣṇa consciousness be accepted by every living entity in the universe. So we will endeavor to execute your order in our fallen condition and always expect your mercy. Please give us shelter at your lotus feet.

(Written by Pṛthuśravā dāsa )

*Your struggling servants in Chicago, New Sevā-kuyjā.*

## Christchurch

*tṛṇād api su nīcena*  
*taror api sahiṣṇunā*  
*amāninā māna-dena*  
*kīrtanīyaḥ sadā harīḥ*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

When we think of why it was that you personally told us to wear this verse around our necks, we have to conclude that it was because you could see we needed humility more than anything else. Of course you are always our perfect example. On-board the *Jaladuta,* you wrote “I am very unfortunate, unqualified and the most fallen.” You, Śrīla Prabhupāda, are Kṛṣṇa’s very dear devotee! But despite you constant example, for many of us humility remains a forgotten priority.

“Just chant Hare Kṛṣṇa!” was your most urgent request, and to follow we desperately need this humility. But being all-merciful and recognizing our deficiency, you also gave many instructions on how to know who you are. When you come to this point of thinking, ‘I am the servant of Kṛṣṇa,’ then the ego is all right.”

So, to remember that we are eternally Kṛṣṇa’s tiny servants is the perfection of our lives; then we can constantly chant Hare Kṛṣṇa, as you did, for we all know from experience that to walk anywhere else than in your firm footsteps is very lonely and leads to only misery. Perhaps, Śrīla Prabhupāda, in this way, we can one day catch up to your eternal residence, Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet!

All glories to Your Divine Grace!

*From the devotees of Christchurch Yatra,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Ālalanātha dāsa / Hṛṣīkeśa dāsa

**Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Dharma dāsa / Yañna dāsa / Bhaktirasa dāsa / Hiraṇyagarbha dāsa / Prāṇanātha dāsa / Rantideva dāsa / Vīśvarūpa dāsa / Janapāvana dāsa / Bhakta Aley / Bhakta Terry / Bhakta Martin / Bhakta Carlos / Bhakta Brent / Viṣṇupadī-devī dāsī / Mīna-devī dāsī / Yamalārjuna-devī dāsī / Kandarpa Mañjarī-devī dāsī / Śyāmā-Sakhī-devī dāsī / Jaladharī-devī dāsī / Taittirīya dāsī / Bhaktin Jill / Bhaktin Stella / Bhaktin Manjula / Bhaktin Anne

## Cleveland

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. As the years go by we are realizing more and more the multitudinous gifts you have given us. Like a treasure chest waiting to be unearthed, so also are the realizations and increased preaching potency that you transmit to us through your books and letters.

Now we are here in this small preaching center, trying to give these gifts back to you for your transcendental pleasure; through increasing the hearing and chanting of those who come, by distribution of *prasadam* via cooking classes, and by planting your seeds at the area colleges.

You have said that spiritual success comes only to those who are able to please their spiritual master (and you, our grand-spiritual master), thereby obtaining their mercy. So now our job is simply to follow your instructions page by page, word by word, and shower you with the results of the gifts you have so mercifully given to us.

*Your insignificant servants,*  
Rāmavīrya dāsa / Divyānanda-devī dāsī

## Copenhagen

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*Please accept our most humble and reverential obeisances prostrated in the dust of the lotus feet of Your Divine Grace, the founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness and the Bhaktivedanta Book Trust.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

You built a house, a most wonderful house. It is very big and stretches beyond national boundaries and oceans. It encompasses the whole world. Yes, you have built a house big enough for the people of the world to live in, the true United Nations, whereby people of all races, creed, and color, and whether male of female, young or old, can take shelter of, living peacefully and harmoniously together and realizing the goal of the human form of life—going back home, back to Godhead.

In this house, you have created so many varieties of activities to keep us engaged. The inhabitants of this house are from such diverse backgrounds that under normal conditions it would have been impossible for them to live and work peacefully and cooperatively together. Others have tried to build such a house—the League of Nations, United Nations(you called it Divided Nations), to name a few, but they have all failed miserably due to diverse and conflicting interests.

But this house is working under a different principle. You revealed the secret formula by which everyone, despite apparent differences, can work for a common interest. You enlighten us as to the eternal nature of the soul—our real identity. We are not these bodies, neither are we the supreme controllers nor enjoyers. We are eternally part and parcel of God, our common father. Our common interest (and real interest) is to serve the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who is the supreme controller, the supreme enjoyer, and the supreme benefactor and well-wisher of all living entities. You dovetailed our various propensities in the service of Kṛṣṇa: “Unity in diversity.” Throughout the years men have been attracted to take shelter of this house, but, unfortunately, many have also left due to forgetting this golden rule.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have built this wonderful house by the strength of your devotion and austerity. You have left us with a great responsibility to maintain this house and to fill it up. The masterplan you have given us. It is indeed a lifetime’s (or many lifetimes’) work. It is no small job for just a single person or a group. It behooves us all to work cooperatively and diligently together to accomplish this herculean task. “Your love for me will be shown by how much you work together.” This is certainly a great test for us.

You left us so soon, and we never had the fortune of meeting you, but we are not bereft of your association. “He lives forever by his divine instructions, and the follower lives with him.” Your Bhaktivedanta purports, innumerable letters and lectures continue to enlighten us and illumine our path. Every day we have your *darśana,* hearing you elaborate on the eternal messages of Kṛṣṇa consciousness so profoundly and with great clarity. Your contributions to humanity are unsurpassed in history, and we are eternally grateful and indebted. Your books are the law books for society for the next ten thousand years. Every day countless unfortunate souls become fortunate by coming into contact with you. Your *vāṇī* is infiltrating every spectrum of society, and people’s lives are affected. Just by reading a word, appreciating the pictures, or even just touching your book, which is like *cintāmaṇi,* their lives are transformed. *Sādhu-saṅga, sādhu-saṅga sarva-śastra kaya / lava-mātra sādhu-saṅga sarva siddhi haya.* So powerful is your influence and so powerful are your books that, despite your physical absence, your presence here in this house is still strongly felt. You remain as our driving and uniting force, our inspiration. Your teachings and instructions are our guiding light, and they are keeping your sincere followers working cooperatively together to fulfill your vision and goals for this extraordinary house.

We are trying too, in our humble ways, to practice your teachings and carry out your instructions to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are neophytes in devotional service and do not have much realization and understanding of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We know, at least, that pleasing the spiritual master is the key and essence of devotional service. We have heard countless quotes of your desire and great pleasure in seeing books being produced and distributed profusely. (This is, after all, the very basis upon which your masterplan for the successful completion of your house is based. We cannot imagine a Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement without your books! It’s horrifying to even think of it.) This has also been constantly drummed into our thick heads by your spiritual son and representative, Harikeśa Swami our spiritual master. We are not very expert devotees, but we feel that if we can simply dedicate our lives to producing and distributing your books, we will be able to attain your mercy. We are very hopeful because, as you have said, the grandfather is more merciful towards the grandsons than the father, and we need all the mercy available. In this way, we may be able to also contribute to the spreading of this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement and the glories of its founder-*ācārya,* Oṁ Viṣṇupāda Paramahāṁsa Parivrājak-*ācārya* Aṣṭottara-śata Śrī Śrīmad A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. Just as Lord Ramacandra was equally pleased with the efforts of the spider as compared to those of the monkeys, we pray that our spiderlike efforts will similarly attract your attention our way.

Falling at your lotus feet, we beg to remain eternally the servants of your servants.

*Your granddisciples from Copenhagen Yatra,*

Cirañjīva dāsa / Haṁsavapu dāsa / Jaya Nṛsiṁha dāsa / Lalitānātha dāsa / Kaṁsahantā dāsa / Narayana dāsa / Utkala dāsa / Yadunandana dāsa / Bhakta Lars Peter / Bhakta Mansa / Bhakta Morten / Aprameyā-devī dāsī / Candrāvalī-devī dāsī / Kaṁsāri-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Marie Louis

## Crescent City, California

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for bringing Kṛṣṇa consciousness to America. Without your mercy we would all be rotting in the material world.

*Your servants in Crescent City, California,*  
*Guru*-caraṇa dāsa / Campaka-latā dāsa / Garuḍa dāsa / Alberto and Louise / Bhakta David and the residents of Crescent City

## Czechoslovakia

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. You are the true representative of the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, who has walked on this earth to save so many fallen souls, including myself, although I had not the slightest desire to be delivered from my suffering condition.

Of course, I realize that it can never be possible to thank you sufficiently for having come and saved me from hell and for engaging me in Lord Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana’s* movement. As a new devotee, I am just beginning to find my place in spiritual life, and only by your special kindness am I avoiding very dangerous pitfalls at every step.

I am an immature devotee, and I can hardly glorify you and your wonderful ISKCON. Sometimes people describe you as “one in a million” or “one in a hundred million,” but that is some way underestimating your greatness. So far in history nobody has managed to single-handedly implant Vedic spiritual culture in a completely strange environment like the Western materialistic countries. Nobody created devotees of Kṛṣān from people who were previously devoted to non-Vedic practices. By your mercy this process is going on even after your so-called “departure.”

One who knows Kṛṣṇa knows everything, and one who knows your books knows Kṛṣṇa; thus your books provide all the knowledge to guide the world. In my service I can often experience this. In Czechoslovakia, the candidates for spiritual life have very little or practically no contact with the regular ISKCON, which means they cannot attend morning programs in the temple room filled up with devotees, cannot hear daily classes given by senior Vaiṣṇava, cannot respect *prasadam* offered to the Deities on the alter, cannot engage their talents in direct service to the spiritual master, and cannot take part in street *saṅkīrtana* without being prosecuted. There is one thing, however, in which they can engage to their hearts content, and that is reading your books and associating with you in this transcendental way. As you have said, you will remain alive in your books. I can see how much truth there is in this statement. Although handicapped in so many ways, the *bhaktas* in Czechoslovakia are developing the Vaiṣṇava qualities and love for Kṛṣṇa only by studying your books, which means that you are there with them, guiding them to achieve the perfection of life.

I would like to quote a few statements from different people who came in contact with your books, which means that you are there with them, guiding them to achieve the perfection of life.

I would like to quote a few statements from different people who came in contact with your books and felt a change of heart. Their appreciation is meant for you, and I felt this is the time to present them to you:

“I just finished reading the biography about Śrīla Prabhupāda, and it is a fantastic book about the true saint. He gave me directions how to live and what is the purpose of life.”

“Recently I borrowed your *Bhagavad-gītā* from my friend, and I was amazed how much brilliance and peace it brought into my life. Can you please send me a copy so that I can study this sublime test at any time?”

“By the causeless mercy of Śrī Kṛṣṇa I was allowed to read *Śrī Isopanisad, Bhagavad-gītā* and *Golden Avatar.* These books brought light and happiness into my life and changed my view of the world.”

“Thank you very much for sending me your books, which were missing in my life. These are the kind of books which are needed in our society so that people can become purified.”

“Recently I finished reading *Śrī Isopanisad,* and the depth and value of this book cannot be expressed in words. I felt like a human being who satisfied his thirst after a long journey in the desert.”

Now, on this most auspicious day of your holy Vyāsa-pūjā, I can only try to rededicate myself to being always in your service as a part of your ISKCON movement. Please let me always serve you by being usefully engaged in your preaching family, so that I may eventually make a worthwhile contribution, which will really please you.

*Hoping to remain a loyal son to you always,*  
*In your service,*  
Turīya dāsa / Bhakta Tomas / Bhakta Radek / Bhakta Petr / Bhakta Roman / Bhakta Karel / Bhaktin Alice

## Dallas

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to your lotus feet. This is the most auspicious occasion of the anniversary of your appearance within this world. We would like to offer you our words of appreciation for you are so kindly giving us unqualified souls the greatest of all benedictions, Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We know that we are very lowly. We cannot very profoundly glorify you, but we pray that our attempt to present you this Vyāsa-pūjā offering may be crowned with the success of pleasing you. Just as Lord Ramacandra was pleased when the little spider brushed some dust into the Indian Ocean to help in the construction of the bridge to Lanka, we are hoping that you will be pleased with our tiny attempt to glorify you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you have requested us to chant Hare Kṛṣṇa and be happy. We are following your instruction and finding the results to be wonderful. *The Nectar of Devotion* is full of sublime instructions on how to literally connect with Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa through so many wonderful means. You have asked us to rise early in the morning and observe the *maṅgala-ārati* ceremony, along with Tulasī worship, *japa, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* class, and *guru-puja.* Those of us who are strictly following your order in this regard are every day experiencing a very sublime taste.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are hankering for that day when we can again sit at your lotus feet and hear you preach the message of the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.* We want to be able to accompany you on your morning walk, eagerly extending our ears to catch every drop of nectar which emanates from your transcendental mouth. Thinking of you, we became wonderfully inspired to carry on cooperatively this most glorious movement. We know that this combined effort is the method which will make us successful. By your dedicating your life to the fulfillment of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhanta Sarasvati’s order, the entire course of human history has been changed. Now by our surrendering to you and cooperating with each other, we can, all of us, play an important part in this respiritualization of the human society.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we know that we can never repay you for the unlimited mercy you are constantly bestowing upon us. However, you have shown us that the best thing we can do is to spread this same mercy of Lord Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu by preaching and distributing your books. We are experiencing a divine intoxication whenever we get the opportunity to be thus engaged. This is giving us great impetus to go on preaching and distributing your books. We are experiencing a divine intoxication whenever we get the opportunity to be thus engaged. This is giving us great impetus to go on preaching this great Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement. Lord Caitanya has asked us all to become *gurus* by preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness to everyone that we meet. This is not always easy for us because we are still foolishly holding on to our material desires and becoming frustrated again and again when they are not satisfied. But gradually, as we become more and more absorbed in spreading this *saṅkīrtana* movement, we see how these material desires are going away. You have given us for our *prabhu-datta-deśa* (place of residence given by the spiritual master) the Dallas / Ft.Worth Metroplex, a true capital of the Kali-yuga. However, we see that so many people here are coming into contact with the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement through our restaurant, our book distribution, our college preaching, and our Indian community preaching. Soon we expect that Kali will be conspicuous by his absence here, and that the happy days of *bhagavata-dharma* will be ushered in with full vitality.

(Written by Saṅkarṣaṇa dāsa)

## Denmark Farm

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
Please accept our most humble obeisances in the dust of your divine lotus feet.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Even though Your Divine Grace never visited Denmark, and we, your granddisciples, unfortunately never got the opportunity to meet you personally, we still feel very much connected with you through your wonderful books. We try to center our whole lives around your books by reading them daily, translating them, and distributing them as much as possible. In this way we are trying to worship and glorify you.

You said that your books are the best part of you, and by studying them carefully one gets to know you and your limitless compassion for all the fallen conditioned souls very easily. While reading your books one cannot avoid becoming infected by your causeless mercy for all living entities and a desire to assist you in trying to please the previous *ācāryas.* The emergency of your mission is apparent on every page, and only a dull-headed, stonelike creature would not want to take it up with great enthusiasm.

*Your fallen servants from the Danish farm,*

Janeśvara dāsa / Daśāvatāra dāsa / Payonidhi dāsa / Pañcamukha dāsa / Jahnu dāsa / Hāḍāī-Ojha dāsa / Bhakta Torben / Bhakta Jens / Kṛṣṇa-bhakti-devī dāsī / Mānasa-candra-devī dāsī / Lavaṅga-latikā-devī dāsī / Ayati-devī dāsī / Ānanda-mayī-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Jenny / Bhaktin Sabine / Arjuna / Acintya / Nityānanda / Kirta

## Denver

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet.

*oṁ ajñāna-timirāndhasya jñaṇañjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlitaṁ yena tasmai śri-gurave namah*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, it seems that for many of us as individuals, and also as a movement, we have reached a critical junction. Kṛṣṇa states in the *Bhagavad-gītā, ye yathā māṁ prapadyante / tāṁs tathaiva bhajāmy aham.* As we surrender unto Him, He rewards us accordingly. He does not reveal Himself or empower us to serve Him mechanically or indiscriminately. This mercy is reciprocal and directly related to our surrender, particularly through the via medium of our spiritual master and his mission.

He also warns us if we want something other than Him, He will give us the faith to strengthen us in the pursuance of that goal, however great or petty it may be. *Tasya tasyācalāṁ śraddhām tām eva vidadhāmy aham.* Yet, He advises us that if we truly desire a spiritual reward, we must be discriminate and narrow in our interests.

*vyavasāyātmikā buddhir*  
*ekeha kuru-nandana*  
*bahu-śākhā hy anantāś ca*  
*bahu-śākhā hy anantāś ca*  
*buddhayo 'vyavasāyinām*  
*Bg 2.41*

**“Those who are on this path are resolute in purpose, and their aim is one, O beloved child of the Kurus, the intelligence of those who are irresolute is many branched.” Even if we begin on the path of devotion, without perseverance the goal may not be attained.”**

*aśraddadhānāḥ puruṣā*  
*dharmasyāsya parantapa*  
*aprāpya māṁ nivartante*  
*mṛtyu-saṁsāra-vartmani*  
*Bg 9.3*

**“Those who are not faithful in this devotional service cannot attain Me, O conqueror of enemies. Therefore they return to the path of birth and death in this material world.”**

Therefore, at every stage of life we must be prepared to choose the more narrow path. Māyā’s job is to offer us those temptations that will distract us from Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet and from perfect surrender unto you, Kṛṣṇa’s representative. Yet, we must resist these false allurements and choose carefully and intelligently the proper, most direct path. “In the process of surrender unto the Supreme Personality of Godhead there are six items: to accept everything favorable for devotional service, to reject everything unfavorable for devotional service…” (NOD pp. 312-313)

not recommended that such strict rule as no outside living should be enforced, but living in the association of devotees is better….I think you should live in the company of the devotees there, to train them and work with them for distributing our books and magazines and pushing on this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement full time….Preaching is our first-class engagement, including *saṅkīrtana* party, selling books, speaking, like that….Whatever position in life one holds he can serve Kṛṣṇa in that way. **But you yourselves being qualified and experienced preachers, what is the benefit of engaging in the second-class activity when there is so much preaching work to be done?** Practically speaking our Kṛṣṇa consciousness philosophy will save the whole world from the most dangerous position, that is a fact. So now you just become convinced yourselves of this fact, and help me spread this movement for the world with all conviction and attention, and in this way soon you will go back to home, back to Godhead, know it for certain.” (Letter December 1971—bold added)

Prabhupāda, we all retain desires for independence. We all desire *lakṣmī* for more than the “bare minimum necessities” you ordered us to accept. We are all over concerned with the health of our rapidly aging and deteriorating bodies. We all are worried over securing our position in this volatile world. We all want more time and material facility to provide nicely for our children, in the hope that they may become devotees.

But we also want Kṛṣṇa. Please help us to not err when faced with a choice. Śrīla Prabhupāda, please let us not be distracted. Let some of us, at least, have this vision of preaching. Let us take preaching the message of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu as our heart and soul. Let us reject pulling of Māyā’s allurements.

“What is the benefit of engaging in the second-class activity when there is so much preaching work to be done?”

You came to save us all conditioned souls, Śrīla Prabhupāda. You gave us your books. You gave us ISKCON. You gave us Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Govinda, the holy name, *prasadam,* and Kṛṣṇa in so many other wonderful forms. And you pulled us out of illusion and gave us faith in God and the spiritual master. You shaved our heads, gave us *dhotis,* drums, book bags, and taught us to see through the eyes of *śāstra.* You made us aspire to be preachers and distributors of the holy name of Kṛṣṇa.

Please bless us now, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that we may keep your *vāṇī* alive*.* Let at least some of us try to set an example of renunciation. Please bless us with the strength to always challenge ourselves, our Godbrothers, and the world, by asking, as you asked, “What is the benefit of engaging in the second-class activity when there is so much preaching work to be done?”

*Your servants in Denver and Boulder,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

## Dhaka, Bangladesh

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*Please accept our humble obeisances.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we should praise you every day, at every moment. Every morning in *maṅgaāratī* and *guru-puja,* in the Gāyatrī *mantras,* before class or taking *prasada,* on resting and rising, plus every time we offer obeisances, we offer you prayers—but now, under the pressure of writing a Vyāsa-pūjā offering, I’m having to think seriously, at last, about who you are and what you mean to us all.

Admittedly (there’s no use pretending—I might be able to fool others, but I certainly can’t fool you), my consciousness is dull. Therefore, to praise you, which should be the spontaneous function of the tongue, now seems like hard work. But even if reams of eloquence fell off my pen, that wouldn’t fool you, either. The real *guru-puja* is the sincere mood of service—an offering of our lives, day after day, minute after minute.

Externally, I may appear to be doing that—after all, I’m still in the mainstream of ISKCON, which should be a cause of smugness, going by percentages. But apart from that fact, I have very little to offer you. My spiritual progress is, at best zero. For how many years will I have to lament like this at Vyāsa-pūjā time? Will I have to go through many lifetimes like this?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, apart from my own rotten condition, I am also worried for the movement you gave us. ISKCON was built with your blood, sweat, and tears—but now we don’t have the dynamism, mood, and sense of purpose of those tears—but now we don’t have the dynamism, mood, and sense of purpose of those “Prabhupāda’s days.” It’s a changing movement in a changing world. Without your personal guidance we’re often unsure on which path to tread. The dangers of compromise, complacency, alteration, outright deviation, and -as always-plain old sex desire, whirl and swirl like dangerous waves to rock the boats of our good intentions.

And what of the future? Even now, we fear pseudo-*gurus* who take your name as a masthead while propagating all kinds of nonsense to increase their own name, fame, and glory. Will we see family lines develop, with devotees claiming privilege due to the achievements of their forefathers? Will we become concerned with external formalities, forgetting the goal of life—to become mad after Kṛṣṇa? Or, worse still, adopt the philosophy that “It’s OK if you don’t follow the regulative principles; just be a devotee anyway.”

History shows that after the disappearance of a great religious teacher, so many sub sects spring up partially professing his teachings and following his practices, but all more or less deviated from the actual path he taught. Right here in Bangladesh we have to struggle against so many nonsense pseudo-Vaiṣṇava leading people to hell in the name of Caitanya Mahāprabhu (although unfortunately some of the men within our camp are also influenced by them.) *Sahajiyās,jātāgosais,* imitation incarnations, purveyors of “new” “*Mahā-mantras”* professional kirtaneers, professional *gurus,* professional reciters of *Bhagavad-gītā,* singers who “howl” (that’s the term you used) in the name of chanting, in a perverse imitation of separation from Kṛṣṇa, but who follow their performance with a cigarette then go home to eat fish—you name it, we’ve got them all. Today Bangladesh, tomorrow the world?

We *must* follow *your* path *strictly*—otherwise we’ll just be missionaries of neo-Hinduism. We must preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness as you have given it to us. We must give those joining this movement a full opportunity to develop pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness by training them to become conversant with your books and your ideas. Otherwise, in your name we’ll be cheating them.

Kṛṣṇa consciousness is your gift to the world. We must live it, experience it, and give it to others. Such transcendental religion always exists far above mundane piety and moral principles. That Kṛṣṇa consciousness you have given us as it is, completely, perfect. Your spiritual master, in what would have been a lifetime’s research for a lesser genius, thoroughly studied all the Vaiṣṇava traditions and methods of practice, and, with intelligence directly illuminated by Kṛṣṇa, gave us Kṛṣṇa consciousness for the modern age—“Bhakti-siddhanta.”

You brought that message intact to the West. If (it’s almost unthinkable) you hadn’t come, then cats, dogs, and monkeys as we were, we would never have had a chance to know of Kṛṣṇa or to save ourselves from hell. You presented one-hundred-percent-genuine Kṛṣṇa consciousness in such a palatable way as to be attractive to those whom others—big Godbrothers and scholars—never cared to give a chance to. The results were wonderful. That we made such a mess of it doesn’t take a glimmer off your glories. Rather, it serves to clarify the difference between a real *ācārya* and any dozens of imitation glowworms.

So we’ve got the perfect package from you, and though we may research into the history of our *sampradaya,* translates the words of our *sampradaya-ācāryas,* and even write our own books (none of which are outside your mandate), still, we must always remember that your books are the lawbooks for ISKCON, and that your way of doing things is our way of doing things. We have no access to any aspects of Kṛṣṇa consciousness except through the understanding and path you have given us.

Sometimes great *ācāryas* appear to say contradictory things, or teach Kṛṣṇa consciousness in different ways and on different levels according to time, place, and circumstance. However, there need be no misunderstanding—you are our *ācārya* (founder- *ācārya* of ISKCON— the *sampradaya-ācārya* for ten thousand years). What you say is *śāstra.* Kṛṣṇa is directly speaking through you. “If you think I have made a mistake, you have made a mistake.” If anyone goes outside your teachings or practices, he is to be rejected, that’s all. No confusion, no Frogism. This is the way of *ācāryas. Yasya deve parā bhaktir….*

Our only hope—as always—is your instructions. Clear, simple, and direct, your words are Vaikuṇṭha-katha, which stab through all material coverings and enter the hearts of receptive devotees.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are struggling. In our hearts, we really desire to surrender to you, but we are compromised by *māyā.* After so many years, we have no better prayer to you than to please force your way into our minds, and drag us, even though we resist, to the lotus feet of your beloved Kṛṣṇa. If it wasn’t for Vyāsa-pūjā, I wouldn’t have thought to say all these things, but now I have been forced to write, I realize that, after all, my heart’s desire is to serve you.

This is a very personalized offering, but anyway, I’m offering it on behalf of the devotees here in Bangladesh. Considering the circumstances, your movement had done fairly well here, though there are definitely vast improvements needed in some areas. My promise to you is that, while I’m in Bangladesh, I’ll try to get the initiated devotees at least to read your books—which they all need to do a lot more than they are presently doing.

Thank you very much for all you have given us, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Please forgive me yet again for being such a sham devotee (how many times will I have to beg for forgiveness—how many times will you forgive me?).

Praying to come alive and sometime develop attachment for your lotus feet.

*On behalf of the devotees in Bangladesh, your servant,*  
Ilāpatidāsa

## Dublin

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Dear Śrīla Prahupada,

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

After meeting some of your followers at Māyāpur, we would like to express some words of gratitude for what you are doing for us in the Dublin temple. Somehow you have kept many of us, your granddisciples, together and in our humble way we are endeavoring to enact your life-giving programs in a way that will be pleasing to you.

We want you to guide us and administer to us from your *asana* in our temple. We want you to accept our offerings of fruit and flowers, so that we can distribute your *prasadam* far and wide. Above all we want you to make all our activities secondary to your desire for book distribution, so that by your grace we may establish your book distribution as an irrevocable fact.

*The servants of your servants in the Dublin Temple,*

**Disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Tulasī-priya dāsa

## Durban

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

You write, and say, “Just like Kṛṣṇa can be present simultaneously in millions of places, similarly the spiritual master also can be present wherever the disciple wants.”

As your disciples, we need now more than ever your association, your guidance, and your firm direction. By reading your purports and letters, listening to your tapes, we can get your association. You write, and say, “You are all limbs of my body.” Each devotee displays some remembrance of you; they each have some little spark of your splendor. So let me stay tightly in the association of your strict followers; let me learn how to serve them, associate with them, cooperate with them and appreciate them. What is the meaning of honoring you if I cannot honor your disciples! The more genuine appreciation and transcendental love I can have for your strict followers, the more I can taste the sublime qualities of your own association. You write, and say, “One should not forget himself as a humble servant although one is addressed as Prabhu. One should never forget to be humble in dealings. If every one of us would conduct our business in that spirit of Prabhu and servant, then there is very little chance of being misunderstood.” (*Letters from Śrīla Prabhupāda,* Vol. 1. p.254)

It seems the basis of our relationships is to be humble. You further write, “A devotee should always remain humble and meek, especially to the authorities and devotees. The more one becomes the lower grade servant of the Lord, the more he is devoted nearer to the Lord. This is our philosophy.” (*Letters from Śrīla Prabhupāda,* Vol. 1, p. 309)

Lord Caitanya’s philosophy is not to become God, but to become servant, servant, servant of God—strong words written by Your Divine Grace, especially in this present atmosphere of unsubmissiveness and individualism. “We should learn to forget and forgive minor incidences because wherever there are two men in a place there is always some misunderstanding. Such misunderstanding happens between husband and wife… what to speak of others.” (*Letters from Śrīla Prabhupāda.* Vol. 1, p. 276)

And finally you talk of appreciation: “Your appreciation for the service of your Godbrothers is very much laudable. This is actually a devotee’s business that everyone should appreciate the value of other devotees. Nobody should criticize anyone. Because everyone is engaged in the service of the Lord, according to one’s capacity, and the thing is, Kṛṣṇa wants to see how much one is sincere in rendering Him service. Materially, we may think that his service is greater that his, that is our material vision.” (*Letters from Śrīla Prabhupāda,* Vol. 1, p. 484)

I humbly pray that on this auspicious occasion of your appearance day, you can inspire your disciples, granddisciples, and followers in the South Africa Yatra to realize the significance of these divine statements and put them into practice.

## El Salvador

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

O beloved spiritual master please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

As it is established in the Vedic literatures, one who is not taught by a bona fide spiritual master cannot understand the Vedic literature. Lord Kṛṣṇa emphasizes this point while instructing Arjuna. He says that because Arjuna is His devotee and confidential friend, he is able to comprehend the mystery of the revealed scriptures, he must accept a bona fide spiritual master, inquire from him submissively, and render service unto him. As it is established in the *Śvetāśvatara Upaniṣad* (6.23),

*Yasya deva parā bhaktir*  
*Yathā deve thathā gurau*  
*Tasyaite kathitā hy arthāḥ*  
*Prakāśante mahāmannaḥ*

**“Only unto those great souls who have implicit faith in both the Lord and the spiritual master are all the imports of Vedic knowledge automatically revealed.”**

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are tired of material existence, and we don’t want to be kicked by *māyā* anymore. We are almost dead; however, the desire to enjoy apart from Kṛṣṇa still prevails in our minds.

Therefore we are prone to perish as sparks perish when they are apart from fire. However, Lord Kṛṣṇa is so merciful to us that He always wants to help us by sending you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because only by your mercy will we be able to get rid of this dark ignorance of material existence. In your eloquent way you have shown us the example of Lord Caitanya Mahāprabhu by chanting the *Mahā-mantra,* Hare Kṛṣṇa, by distributing

*Prasada,* and by profusely preaching of the message of *Bhagavad-gītā* and *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.* We are completely convinced of your exalted position as a perfect *ācārya* and bona fide representative of Śrī Kṛṣṇa, the Supreme Personality of Godhead.

Therefore, dear spiritual father, please accept this humble offering on Vyāsa-pūjā and permit us to very humbly take shelter under the shadow of your divine lotus feet.

*Your fallen, but fortunate, servants of San Salvador*

## Fiji

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, here in Fiji, most of us have never seen you personally, yet, by your mercy, the ISKCON Fiji Yatra is progressing well. Somehow, you dropped a transcendental seed of Lord Caitanya’s mission. This spiritual seed has taken root and is now beginning to develop strong stems, healthy leaves, and shoots. The proof of this has been more centers, more dedicated devotees, and steadiness and cooperation among devotees, even during the trying political disruption in Fiji. In general, the whole population became fearful, feeling insecure. But those who had taken shelter of your Kṛṣṇa consciousness instructions remained unaffected, knowing that the entire material world is full of danger—*pada pada yad vipadāṁ na teṣām.* Now, we feel further convinced that this material world, including Fiji, is not a paradise.

During your last days of physical presence in this world, you expressed a lot of concern for Fiji. The Fiji Yatra was in its infancy stage, so naturally you were like a very concerned father. We feel very appreciative for your concern and always feel confident of your loving glance. Five thousand years ago Kṛṣṇa had sent Kaliya to these remote islands. But when you came, you brought remembrance of Kṛṣṇa’s pastimes by reestablishing Kṛṣṇa, the transcendental hero, on the hoods of the Kaliya serpent, In this way, you give us the opportunity to serve Him at the Śrī Kṛṣṇa-Kaliya Temple and spread His glories.

Not just for Fiji, Śrīla Prabhupāda, but we see that you had great concern for spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness all over the world. Your untiring and unceasing endeavors were the proof of your pure desire. Above all, your genuine desire to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness without cheating or ulterior motives is clearly visible in the thousands of letters to you wrote to your disciples and others.

Your contribution towards the welfare of society far surpasses all contributions of others put together. Many people, including many leaders of the world, made various contributions, but none of them ever had the knowledge, ability, and courage to deal with the decaying root. You alone stand tall like a tower, with your disciples, temples, teachings, library of books, and personal example attacking the rotting hollow in society—lack of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Ignoring this basic necessity, others’ contributions are built on false foundations and are thus destined to crumble, creating chaos and dissatisfaction in society.

The subject of various Vedic processes and their differing results, which even great, great *munis* are unable to comprehend throughout their lives, has been explained by you in such a simple way that even ordinary people are able to understand it easily. You taught and inspired all types of people, though born in Vedic culture, are unable to understand and follow. This ability of yours to change people’s lives is like that of Lord Caitanya, who made the animals of Jhārikhāṇḍa sing and dance in ecstasy. People of Kali-yuga had unfortunately reached the level of animals. To uplift human beings from such a degraded level is even more remarkable-*ye ’nye ca pāpā yad-apāśrayāśrayāḥ / sudhyanti tasmai prabhavisnave namaḥ.* Even those addicted to sinful acts can be purified by taking shelter of the devotees of the Lord Kṛṣṇa, and His devotees are able to purify all types of degraded people. This is the real proof of your pure devotion to Kṛṣṇa, your unlimited compassion, and your most valuable contribution to society.

One Calcutta billboard reads—“Prabhupāda, Calcutta’s Greatest Son.” But we say that you are “The World’s Greatest Son,” because you worked indiscriminately for the welfare of all.

Please accept our homage and continue to bestow your blessings on all your disciples, granddisciples, and others who come to take shelter of your lotus feet.

*Your servants in Fiji,*

## Gainesville College Preaching Center

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda. All glories to your lotus feet.

Since your departure we have been struggling to fully act on your instruction: “Differences may be there, but still you have to cooperate together.” We are glad to report that there has been significant progress on this front. Recently, some favorable scholars were asked if the difficulties in ISKCON disturbed their confidence in your movement. On the contrary, they were amazed that ISKCON was so viable a decade after your departure! Another, scholar analyzed the method of organization of the GBC and stated that historically it is the only successful method of succession in religious movements.

Though we have had many controversies, the central point has always, been to please you and to follow your instructions—and that we are learning to do. We, in Gainesville, are at a college preaching center. Our goal is to have the entire academic community appreciate and glorify your books. We start this process with ample *prasadam* distribution to give them an inoculation against *māyā,* we chant, and finally we introduce the philosophy. In this way the whole campus community begins to appreciate how the Hare Kṛṣṇas are transcendentalists. Then we show them your books ( by you, about you, and by your followers), and at that point, if they know it or not, they’ve been captured.

Our task is seemingly impossible—to change the hearts of living entities in this Kali-yuga. Yet you came to this materialistic place (you referred to Gainsville in your talk here in 1971 as a very remote place) and changed so many hearts that we can only pray to be your strict followers and to spread your Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement.

*Your unworthy servants,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Brahmatīrtha dāsa / Bhakti-devī dāsī

**Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Dāru-brahma dāsa / Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa dāsa / Lalita-Mādhava-devī dāsī

**Children**

Kīrtana / Baladeva / Kṛṣṇa-darśana

## Gallarate

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. As time goes on and this body gets older, it becomes evident how far away is the goal of life. Also, the juvenile ideas of easily obtainable perfection become a remembrance of the past. How strong is Māyā, and how elevated you are in love of God! Any prospect of coming near to you in serving Kṛṣṇa has become ridiculous, and the vital necessity for your merciful instructions is the true realization. Poor understanding, phenomenal attractions, laziness, envy for other living entities, and constant disturbance are my offerings of gratitude to you. Your delicate service to Śrīmati Rādhārani is the most elevated level of existence; still you tolerate the gross and selfish mentality of service that I offer to you. What is the use of my endeavors and sacrifices if I do not become aware of the sublime nature of your compassion and all-time care? Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give me attachment to you, to the *ācāryas,* to your transcendental mission. But for you I have no hope, no shelter, no spiritual desires, no life. Let my dependence on you increase day by day. Never mind the judgment of mundaners, erudite fools, moralists; my body may get older and older, but I want to become more and more a child that anxiously depends on you forever.

*Your disciples and granddisciples from your temple in Gallarate, Italy.*

## Gita-nāgarī

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

You are most dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa because you are actively engaged in preaching the message of the *Bhagavad-gītā* vigorously and tirelessly to the whole world without discrimination. You instructed us that we are not these material bodies, but that we are spirit souls, eternally linked to Lord Kṛṣṇa through service. You have revived within our hearts through the chanting of the Hare Kṛṣṇa *Mahā-mantra* love of God, Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You have instructed us on the great importance of cow protection. Concerning the *vaiśya* class, you have stated in the purport of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,* First Canto, Ninth Chapter, verse 26, “The *vaiśyas,* the members of the mercantile communities, are especially advised to protect the cows. Cow protection means increasing the milk production, namely curd and butter. Agriculture and distribution of the foodstuffs are the primary duties of the mercantile community backed by Vedic knowledge and trained to give in charity. As the *kshatriyas* were given charge of the protection of the citizens, *vaiśyas* were given charge of the protection of animals. Animals are never meant to be killed. Killing of animals is a symptom of barbarian society. For a human being, agricultural produce, fruits, and milk are sufficient and compatible foodstuffs. The human society should give more attention to animal protection. The productive energy of the laborer is misused when he is occupied by industrial enterprises. Industry of various types cannot produce the essential needs of man, namely rice, wheat, grains, milk, fruits and vegetables. The production of machines and machine tools increases the artificial living fashion of a class of vested interests and keeps thousands of men in starvation and unrest. This should not be the standard of unrest.”

All over the world you have instituted cow protection and the distribution of *Kṛṣṇa-prasādam* backed by the authority of the *Vedas,* specifically the *Bhagavad-gītā* and the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam.*

Of Gītā-nāgarī you have written in 1948 or 1949 in an essay titled “Interpretations of the *Bhagavad-gītā,” “*It shall be the foremost business of Gītā-nāgarī to chalk out its plans of action strictly on the teachings of *Bhagavad-gītā* and nothing more and nothing less.”

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are the ever well-wisher of the living entities. You have shown us by your life’s example how to “always remember Kṛṣṇa and never forget Kṛṣṇa.” Your whole life was based on living the *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is.*

We have heard you say in a taped lecture that “the solution to our economic problem is cow protection and agriculture.” So to implement and execute these two programs based on the teachings of the *Bhagavad-gītā* is of paramount concern to the development and advancement of civilization.

“If there is enough milk, enough grains, enough fruit, enough cotton, enough silk, and enough jewels, then why do people need cinemas, houses of prostitution, slaughterhouses, etc.? What is the need of an artificial, luxurious life of cinemas, cars, radio, flesh and hotels? Advancement of civilization is not estimated on the growth of mills and factories, which deteriorate the finer instincts of the human being, but on developing the potent spiritual instincts of the human being, but on developing the potent spiritual instincts of the human being and giving him a chance to go back to Godhead.” (*Bhāg.* 1.10.4, 1.11.12)

Just as in American history there was Johnny Appleseed, who planted apple trees everywhere, you have come on Lord Caitanya’s behalf not only to America but to the whole world and planted the *bhakti-lātā-bīja, “*the seed of devotional service” to Lord Kṛṣṇa. This seed, when watered by the chanting of Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rama, Hare Rama, Rama Rama , Hare Hare, and cultivated by *bhakti-yoga* to keep it from the weeds of materialistic life, will grow into a mature plant bearing love of God, which is the sweetest of all fruits.

So, at Gītā-nāgarī you have instructed us to live a simple village life based on the teaching of the *Bhagavad-gītā.* You have asked us to protect the cow and work the bulls and to produce sufficient foodstuff for large-scale *prasadam* distribution. You have asked us to sit in the lap of material nature and *depend on Kṛṣṇa.* You have planted the seeds of love of God and shown us how to grow, cultivate, harvest, and distribute this sweetest of fruits—love of God.

We beg at your divine lotus feet that we may carry out your orders as our life and soul, to firmly establish Gītā-nāgarī, based cent per cent on the *Bhagavad-gītā As It Is,* and to dynamically spread the principles of cow protection.

We beg to remain you humble servants in your service to Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Damodara and His cows.

*The residents of Gītā-nāgarī*

## Goteborg

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

There are unlimited jewels in your treasure house of *prema-bhakti,* but on this most auspicious day we would like to mention at least one of them.

It is this jewellike quality of yours, to implant faith in the hearts of the innocent. We, the conditioned souls, are struggling hard in this age of hypocrisy and quarrel, with many cheaters, including our senses, mind crippled intelligence, and powerful false ego. These deceitful enemies are followed up by cheating leaders, who are increasing the bodily consciousness of their followers. Many of us are turning out of despair to various impersonalistic , concocted philosophies , thinking that our disappointment cam be removed by negating any personal identity. We hope to find shelter in impersonal concepts, like democracy, socialism, parliamentarianism, and so many other concocted “isms.” However, our nature is personal, and so our desperate struggle continues. Finally, with no alternative left, we become pessimistic and cynical, mechanically repeating some atheistic dogmas.

What a wonder you did, Śrīla Prabhupāda! You picked these helpless souls from the comalike stage of ignorance and filled their hearts with trust. It is this trust which is protecting the seed of *bhakti* you planted, protecting it from the destructive force of doubts called demons. You, the supreme personalist, implanted in our hearts faith in the Supreme Person, Lord Kṛṣṇa. By reflecting so wonderfully and purely the all-attractive nature of God, you attracted us all by your unlimited, merciful arrangements. You tied us up by your boundless kindness, making us feel eternally indebted to Your Divine Grace. It is this faith and trust which is giving us strength to serve you day after day. Your expression of gratitude left us stunned with shame and embarrassment, pushing us again and again to new standards of devotional life. It was your grave look, your strong words, which crashed our pride and false self-confidence.

Now we are standing here on this day, begging Your Divine Grace for shelter and strength. Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, please give us the intelligence to be able to catch at least a spark from this jewellike quality of yours, and reflect upon the suffering conditioned souls. You saved us, and you want to save them, so please give us the strength to penetrate the walls of atheistic impersonalism and give these innocent souls faith in your words. Let us place your books in their homes and faith in Lord Kṛṣṇa in their hearts, so the doubtful Kali-yuga man can give up his despair and share with us the safe shelter of your instructions. Let us replace their relative trust in matter by absolute trust in Your Divine Grace. Please give us the strength to turn our words into action. Thank you very much, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for saving us from cheaters by showing us the reality.

*Bowing down at your lotus feet, your devotees in Goteborg:*

**Disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Mānidhara dāsa

**Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Yaśodā-dulāl dāsa / Haritāśva dāsa / Bhakta mats / Bhakta Shahriar / Bhakta Lars / Bhakta Anders / Bhakta Anders / Bhakta Dick / Mitravindā-devī dāsī

## Greece

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances on this most auspicious day of your appearance.

Some people say that you have left us, that now we are alone. But we can understand that this is just a trick of Māyā. We say this with complete confidence, just as the citizens can say with complete confidence that there is a king by seeing his servants. We know that you are guiding and protecting us because your servants are present before us.

Here in Greece things were looking a little difficult for some time, but now you are sending more and more help. In this situation, we remember Mṛgāri, the hunter, who by following the instructions of Śrī Nārada completely changed, and all his needs were taken care of. You have done the same thing with us—you have picked us up out of the material existence, given us service to the Supreme Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, and all our needs have been taken care of. And to our own surprise, you are also changing our hearts.

Considering all that you are giving us and doing for us, to ask for more might not seem proper. But there is one request we would like to make: Please keep us always engaged in your service, so that we will never again fall into the grips of the illusory energy and forget Kṛṣṇa.

*Your servants in Greece,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Nanda-gopa dāsa / Gokulānanda dāsa
