## Pune

*oṁ ajñana-timirāndhasya jñānāñjana-śalākayā*  
*cakṣur unmīlītaṁ yena tasmai śrī-gurave namaḥ*

**“I was born in the darkest ignorance, and my spiritual master opened my eyes with the torch of knowledge. I offer my respectful obeisances unto him.”**

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvati Gosvami. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya-deva and are delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet, which are the shelter of innumerable fallen souls.

Try as hard as we do to pay our salutations to you, words become scarce to do the same. It is indeed true that we are the most insignificant and fallen of all living entities. We possess no qualification at all and require your causeless mercy to serve you. We also need your constant transcendental association to effectively thwart the attempts of Māyā to ensnare us.

You have given us, the way ward devotees, a sense of direction, for which we are indebted to you. We thank you from the bottom of our heart for saving for saving us from the prison of material existence. Please bless us so that we may act as the medium to spread the transcendental message to the rest of the world.

On this most auspicious day of your appearance, we fervently pray for your blessings to enable us to remain totally engaged in Kṛṣṇa consciousness and constantly endeavoring to preach your glories.

*Your eternal servants from Pune,*

Vrajendra-nandana dāsa / Bhānu dāsa / Baladeva dāsa / Pāvana dāsa / Svarūpa Dāmodara dāsa / Dāmodara dāsa / Mādhav*ācārya* dāsa / Śaunaka Ṛṣi dāsa / Bhakta Perumal / Bhakta Raju / Bhakta Vittal / Sudarśinī-devī dāsī / Krśendriya-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Geeta / Bhaktin Janaki / Bhaktin Narayani / Bhaktin Rādhā / Bhaktin Vrinda / Bhaktin Surekha

## Pyramid Center for Bhakti-yoga and ITV

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. All glories to Your Divine Grace, who enlightens the universe by your invisible mercy, as the sun chases away the darkness by its unseen rays.

*Atho samāthā bhavatā sma yad vayaṁ*  
*Traiviṣtapānām api dūra-darśanam*  
*Prema-smita-snigdha-nirīkṣanānanaṁ*  
*Paśyema rūpaṁ tava sarva-saubhagam*

**“Oh, it is our good luck that we have come again today under Your protection by Your presence, for Your Lordship rarely visits even the denizens of heaven. Now it is possible for us to look into Your smiling faces, which is full of affectionate glances. We can now see Your transcendental form, full of happiness.”(*****Bhāg.*** **1.11.8*****)***

In your purport you state that “The Lord in His eternal personal form can be seen only by the pure devotees….\[He is\] possible to be visited by devotional service face to face.” Whenever we enter your temples and look upon the beautiful Deity form of the Lord, we first offer our respectful obeisances unto you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, for without your pure devotion there would never have been any temples to enter or forms of Kṛṣṇa for us to view. Your love for Kṛṣṇa is the original key which has unlocked the doors to devotional service for us sinful, meat-eating, atheistic rascals immersed in *māyā* and destined to take birth in all kinds of despicable, repulsive bodies and conditions. You are the original embodiment of mercy for the Western *mūḍhas* whom you plucked from the material ocean and to whom you offered a vision of Kṛṣṇa which even the denizens of heaven are standing in line to see.

You are the *ādi-*trendsetter of preaching Vaisnavism to the most fallen, and you will forever be glorified in the hearts of the *caṇḍāla* devotees.

After you, many have come with the message of *bhakti,* but you are number one. Time itself cannot change the order of offering articles to the Lord, nor can it alter the fact that you broke the ice of a New York winter before any *sādhu* of Kṛṣṇa ventured from the soil of Bhārata-varṣa.

*Yad yad ācarati śreṣṭhas*  
*Tat tad evetaro janaḥ*  
*Sa yat pramāṇaṁ kurute*  
*Lokas tad anuvartate*

**“Whatever action is performed by a great man, common men follow in his footsteps. And whatever standards he sets by exemplary acts, all the world pursues.”(Bg. 3.21)**

Now there are other *maṭhas* and temples being developed and constructed around the world, but they all followed you. May we all follow you closely. It is our “good luck”—our great fortune—“that we come again today under your protection….”

Please allow us to serve you and worship you, who are the first even among equals, of which in our mind there are none.

*We beg to remain your fallen but still trying servants.*

Nṛsiṁhānanda dāsa / Siddhānta dāsa / Bhakta Philip / Śacīdulāla dāsa / Jivānanda dāsa / Īśāna / Mohana-devī dāsī / Pāvaka-devī dāsī

## Rajkot

**Śrīla Prabhupāda Viraha**

Let us offer our respectful obeisances to Śrīla Prabhupāda, whose face is more splendid than millions of moons and whose smile is more charming than moon-light. You are the moon who has risen from Lord Caitanya’s ocean of pure love of Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. Oh! When will you again be within our vision?

O Śrīla Prabhupāda, by devotedly serving your lotus feet one can attain pure *Kṛṣṇa-bhakti,* the ultimate goal of our lives. Śrīla Prabhupāda, O auspiciousness of the world, we offer our obeisances unto you. We offer our obeisances millions and trillions of times again and again unto your lotus feet.

Boundlessly merciful, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you who are constantly immersed in tasting the sweet nectar of pure love of Kṛṣṇa gave in charity freely this topmost nectar to us, the lowest. You vanquished all the inauspiciousness of this world. By your presence here the whole world was glorified. O crest jewel of all Kṛṣṇa’s devotees, we offer you our repeated humble obeisances!

What was not attainable by diligent performers of pious activities; what was never understood by those engaged in severe austerities, meditation and *yoga;* what was never guessed at by those absorbed in renunciation, philosophical speculation or recitation of prayers; and what was even unknown to devotees full of love for Lord Kṛṣṇa; that king of all secrets—pure unalloyed devotional service through the holy name—you have revealed to us. All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda! Chanting the Hare Kṛṣṇa *mahā-mantra,* showing the way, creating tidal waves in the ocean of mercy of dynamic, fearless preaching, splendidly dressed in effulgent *sannyāsī* cloth, as glorious as the sunrise and glowing with pure love for Kṛṣṇa may that moon, Śrīla Prabhupāda, rise in our hearts and shine eternally. Where is causeless mercy? Where is fearless shelter? Where is generosity? Where is the tranquil indifference to pleasures and pains? Where are the great waves of humbleness? Where is the intense devotion? Where is the love for devotees? Where is the love for Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa? Where is the constant, dynamic, powerful and fearless preaching? Where are the virtues present like yours, Śrīla Prabhupāda?

Even once seeing Śrīla Prabhupāda’s gentle face overcome with love for Kṛṣṇa, speaking words as sweet as nectar, spitting at insignificant sense “pleasure” of this world, one would never abandon the ocean of mercy that is Śrīla Prabhupāda.

If by churning the ocean of nectar one attains the sweet essence of all nectar, it will still taste bitter to those who have tasted the sweetness of service to the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda! Let renunciation be multiplied millions of times! Let millions of virtues be multiplied millions of times! Let there be devotion to Viṣṇu multiplied millions of times! Yet, all these together can never equal even a millionth of the wonderful qualities of Śrīla Prabhupāda.

By the power of devotional service to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we can dive into the ocean of love for Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. A person untouched by these waves is the poorest of the poor and instead will dive into an ocean of unwanted activities of lifeless birth and death.

Again and again he chants Hare Kṛṣṇa. Again and again he sings Jaya Rādhā Madhava. Again and again he is overcome with ecstatic love for Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you possess the unparalled opulence of nectarean *Kṛṣṇa-prema,* and you are our only opulence.

Pathetic is one’s noble birth! Pathetic is one’s eloquence! Pathetic is one’s fame, his scholarship, his wealth, his handsome, strong, young body, his status as a *brahmana.* Useless is his very breath if he, in this miserable age of Kali, doesn’t receive one of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s books!

O brothers, please take the fearless shelter of the lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda. He is the person who all have been searching for. He is the person who showers millions of nectar oceans of blissful love for Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. He is that person who can melt our hearts and make us lovers of Gaura-Nitāi.

The lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda are more purifying than millions of Ganges Rivers, more cooling and pleasing than millions of desire trees, more affectionate than millions of Bengali mothers, and more merciful than millions of rain clouds. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, when will that day be ours when you will lovingly place your lotus feet in our hearts?

We have all been rescued by your causeless mercy, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you did not consider who was qualified or not, who was your own and who was an outsider. You came to America to save us, the great sinners, the lowest of mankind, dog-eaters, cow-eaters! Who can realize your mercy?

What is the use of studying many scriptures? What is Svargaloka or worthless material opulences? If one’s heart is set on crossing the ocean of death and yearns to swim in the nectar ocean of pure love for Kṛṣṇa, then one should take shelter of Śrīla Prabhupāda, who is dressed in the garments of a *sanyasi* and constantly immersed in that ocean of *Kṛṣṇa-bhakti.* All glories to the effulgent lamp known as Śrīla Prabhupāda! Shining with the oil of transcendental love, you destroyed the darkness in our hearts and illuminated the hidden, thorn-filled, impossible and treacherously winding path that leads to the rarely achieved, swift nectar-ocean of pure love for Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you brought the full nectar to us, who were burning like moths in the cruel fire of material sense gratification. You spoke to us, tolerated us, taught us, gave us your shelter, and revealed the ecstatic festival of pure love of Kṛṣṇa. Those who, despite receiving so much mercy from you, still refuse to serve you eternally must be considered the most foolish, unfortunate.

Now the moon of Śrīla Prabhupāda has uncovered the path of pure *Kṛṣṇa-bhakti,* materialists have stopped talking about themselves, their wives and children; the scholars have stopped debating; the *yogis* have stopped controlling breath; ascetics have given up severe austerities; and the hearts of the cow-eaters, which were as hard as a steel thunderbolts, are melting as soft as butter. Now there is only one sweetness in life—the nectar of loving service to Śrīla Prabhupāda.

In the past, someone with an impure heart twice or thrice sang God’s holy name. That was the past. Now that the moon of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s books has risen, “Hare Kṛṣṇa” is a household word! And thousands of souls can attain priceless pure love of Kṛṣṇa by chanting the holy name. What great good fortune has this world not attained?

In the past, when even the greatest philosophers presented hosts of arguments, on one could be certain who was correct. But now the powerful suns of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s books have arisen, all the darkness of ignorance has been dissipated and we are all certain that pure devotion to Kṛṣṇa is the ultimate meaning of all the *Vedas* and the topmost goal of life. Who had heard the word Kṛṣṇa before? Who had heard the words of *Śrī Radhe* or *pure love for Kṛṣṇa?* Who had heard of the glories of the holy name? Who had ever entered the sweet forests of Vṛndāvana before? Who had ever heard of Caitanya Mahāprabhu? What cow-eater ever cried tears of pure love of Lord Kṛṣṇa before? It is only Śrīla Prabhupāda who has revealed all this to the whole world. All glories to golden Śrīla Prabhupāda!

When Śrīla Prabhupāda appeared in this world, he flooded our lives with nectar— the real nectar of pure love of Kṛṣṇa. There was ecstatic, tumultuous chanting of the holy names everywhere; continuous, ever-increasing book distribution everywhere; dynamic preaching programs everywhere, ecstasy everywhere! Oh, will that sweet time ever come again? When will the golden lotus feet of Śrīla Prabhupāda come within our sight again?

May the fearless shelter of Śrīla Prabhupāda’s lotus feet eternally shine in our hearts and protect us all.

*Your aspiring servants at ISKCON Rajkot, Gujerat*

## Salzburg

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

Humbly we try to stand in front of your worshipable form,  
With garlands of flowers and chosen words,  
Respectfully offering body and soul to you…

You who have come so kindly to lead us,  
You who live always and labor to free us,  
You who eternally strive to relieve us  
From painful illusion and crippled confusion,  
By showering upon us your merciful glances,  
Enlivening our hearts with words filled with truth and purity,  
Delivering us to the path of devotion,  
Engaging us in the mission of surrendering fully to the Lord,  
You constantly give us your nectarean association…

Humbly and helplessly we strive to express  
Our total inability to repay our eternal debt,  
For if it hadn’t been for His Divine Grace,  
Who would have saved us from Yamarāja’s grasp?  
Who else would have cared when we would gasp  
For our last breath in a life meant for death?

You have given us a meaningful existence,  
Free from the contamination of Māyāvadi’s insurgence,  
Free from the lusty and greedy karmī mentality,  
A life filled with hard work paired with bodily and mental sanity.

Humbly and meekly we prostrate our heads at your lotus feet,  
At which countless prabhus in the universe sit.  
We beg to stay alive in your mission  
Of hearing, chanting, remembering,  
Offering obeisances, and worshiping.  
And last, but not least,  
Of translating, producing, and distributing your transcendental books, the best Preaching.

He who has life can preach,  
So we beg, again and again, Your Divine Grace, to keep us alive,  
‘less in the bhava ocean we shall die.

Humbly, without identifying body with soul,  
In your and Lord Caitanya’s army we wish forever to be enrolled.

*Your servants and servants of the servants from Salzburg, Austria,*

Jayagurudeva dāsa / Jīmūta dāsa / Bhakta Peter / Bhakta Joe / Bhakta Karstem / Vṛndāvanī-devī dāsī / Śaḍbhuja-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Uli / Bhaktin Hermine

Śeṣa dāsa / Nanda / Nimāi / Rāmadāsa / Govinda / Dāmodara

## San Diego

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

It is very difficult to truly appreciate what you have given us, but this gradually becomes possible for one who sincerely tries to assimilate your instructions. There are many states of material consciousness in which the conditioned soul can be entangled, and persons caught in particular relative states of consciousness cannot see the larger framework of reality which encompasses their own situation. It is therefore very difficult for them to escape from the prison of their limited conceptions, and normally they rotate through different illusory realms in an evolutionary sequence, carried by the current of Māyā. However, by revealing transcendental knowledge you have brilliantly illuminated the total reality within which our existence takes place, and you have thereby enabled us to understand the highest goal of life.

Some persons see the world as a product of simple, mechanistic processes that can be fully understood by the human mind. Brushing aside all contrary evidence, they build comforting scientific theories that portray human beings as the pinnacle of an impersonal evolutionary process.

Some worship subtle living beings, in categories ranging from ghosts to demi-gods, for the purpose of satisfying material desires. Others profess faith in more exalted systems of religion, but twist their teachings in accordance with sectarian views based on the bodily conception of life. Still others engage in sophisticated intellectual arguments designed to prove that we are all God, and that we can create our own reality.

If a person seeking absolute knowledge surveys the pandemonium of human belief and opinion, it is not surprising that he often becomes totally bewildered and withdrawn within a hard shell of fruitless skepticism. It is therefore remarkable that you have revealed to us a system of knowledge that enables us to systematically understand all the world’s contending philosophies, and at the same time see beyond them to the transcendental principle of pure devotional service to Kṛṣṇa.

Of course, full realization of this knowledge generally does not come immediately. Most of us are still tied in various ways to a limited outlook, and so we have difficulty in appreciating the universal meaning of the Vedic literatures. But we see from direct experience that by studying your brilliant expositions of these literatures, and at the same time engaging in practical expositions of these literatures, and at the same time engaging in practical devotional service in accordance with your instructions, deeper and deeper insights into the Vedic philosophy are regularly revealed. As a result, this regular study does not become dry or hackneyed, and we feel that we have not even begun to exhaust the content of your teachings.

Some may say that the Vedic knowledge is available in the *śāstras* and the teachings of the great *ācāryas,* and so any reasonable learned and dedicated person could have spread it around the world as you have done. However, if this is so, then why is no one presently spreading Kṛṣṇa consciousness with the great boldness and vigor that you displayed? Of the five billion people in this planet, very few have even heard of Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and of those who have heard of it, very few appreciate its real importance. There is much work to do, but in your absence it is proceeding very slowly, in spite of the great momentum that you created.

Actually, to spread Kṛṣṇa consciousness one must have the mercy of Kṛṣṇa, and this mercy is available in proportion to the purity of one’s devotion to Kṛṣṇa. You are certainly a pure devotee of Kṛṣṇa, but we feel that even among pure devotees your position is exceptional. Out of many pure devotees, you were the first to plant the creeper of devotion to Kṛṣṇa in the very countries that pioneered the modern world movement of materialism and atheism, and to do this you had to undergo great personal hardship. Surely you are a unique gift by the mercy of Kṛṣṇa for the salvation of this suffering world.

(Written by Sadāpūta dāsa)

## Santo Domingo

**Part One**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet. We are now in the year of 1988, at scarcely two decades since you first founded the International Society for Krishna Consciousness with a handful of some sincere souls, whom by your grace were allowed participation in pioneering the introduction of Lord Caitanya’s glorious movement for the spreading of Kṛṣṇa consciousness in the rampant materialistic society of the Western world. The magnificent brilliant effulgence of the golden era had thus been unfolded before them.

Thereafter, just as a loving father guides and teaches his little child to give his first steps, in the same way, you kindly and patiently taught them the ABC’s of spiritual life. And you did that by your words, as well as by your very own personal example.

You actually gave them the rare opportunity to become first-class human beings, by making them inheritors of the most sublime and pure knowledge of loving devotion to God.

They were enchanted by your transcendental purity and scholarship, which increasingly made them relish the blissful ecstasies of awakening to their true spiritual selves and their eternal loving relationship with the infinite Lord.

You often used to tell them that the Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is like a boat meant to rescue all fallen conditioned souls from the ocean of material existence. So many got on the boat but later fell aboard. And among those still on board, confusion arose, as you departed from this world. And thinking they were doing right by doing exactly as you had done, some became seriously affected by false pride, while others, whose love for you is transparent and sincere, with great humility and soberness admitted the mistake.

There have been so many discrepancies and fights over so many internal matters, while people on the outside, looking greatly concerned and horrified, struggling amidst the turbulent waves of the fearful ocean, as if desperately imploring, “Please stop the controversies among yourselves and help us get on the boat before it’s too late.”

Where are you now, O great admiral? Are you joyfully enjoying the association of your beloved Lord in the transcendental pasturing grounds of Vrajadhāma? Or are you somewhere in the material universes directing another boatload of conditioned souls back home, back to Godhead?

You came to this particular universe and to this particular planet to carry out the plan of the Lord. You did your job as an obedient order-carrier, without any attachments to the results. Your only business is to eternally glorify His holy names, wherever He may want you to.

And He designated you specifically, among His unlimited number of eternal associates, to fulfill His mission: that in every town and village of this planet, His holy names would be heard!

You are the person envisioned by Śrīla Bhaktivinoda Thākura, who was destined to spread the holy name throughout the whole world. Who can deny it, as evident as it is?

Therefore you are a very special one among many. They gave you the name Prabhupāda as a recognition of this fact. But, was it really them? (Not a blade of grass moves without the sanction of the almighty Lord!)

Ten thousand years of existence seem rather utopian considering the overall look of our worldwide transition through such a fragile and uncertain situation. But this is actually a spiritual movement situated far beyond the jurisdiction of the limiting influence of time and peace. It is Lord Caitanya’s movement. And He is the Supreme Personality of Godhead, by whom all impossible things are made possible. And as His bona fide representative, you knew this perfectly well.

You did everything all by yourself, just as your *Guru* Mahārāja, Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Thākura, had predicted. And the big house you built will certainly be safely upheld by the strong pillars of your instructions, as they are found in your books and in the hearts of those whose very existence is to find solace only at the dust of your lotus feet.

May you always be our Śrīla Prabhupāda, the one and only, whose exalted position no one can encroach upon, for it has been established by Providence. May everyone celebrate you as the savior of the whole world. And with great earnestness, let’s shout out loud: Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda, founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness!

*From your grandsons at Santo Domingo Yatra:*

Yadunanda dāsa / Nakula Brahmacārī dāsa / Mathureśa dāsa / Kṛṣṇa-krpa dāsa / Rāmānanda Rāya dāsa / Madhukānta dāsa / Murāri Caitanya dāsa / Jīvātmā dāsa / Madhumaṅgala dāsa / Līla-śuka dāsa / Gobaṭa dāsa / Madhukānta dāsa / Jagaannātha dāsa / Garuḍa dāsa / Mādhava dāsa / Lakṣmī-Nārāyaṇa dāsa / Śikhī Māhitī dāsa / Gadādhara dāsa / Bhakta Johnny / Bhakta Charlie / Bhakta Mario / Bhakta Elvim / Bhakta Edgar / Bhaktin Rita / Bhakta Josefina / Bhaktin Jacquelyne / Bhaktin Luisa / Bhaktin Milagros / Bhaktin Mary / Bhaktin Margurita / Bhaktin Ivelisse

**Part Two**

Difficult to see from where I stand,  
So dense the smog that covered my mind,  
No hope had I, no future at hand,  
A whole world in front, nothing to understand.

Frustration was my step,  
Ignorance was my line,  
Deceit was my rhythm, and  
Ego was my shrine.

Even though I lost my cool,  
Every time I breathed or smiled  
I still pretended to be the boss,  
And to always be in style.

But deep inside, where lies don’t stand,  
My heart was running out of lines,  
Seclusion was my final round,  
For all my bridges had fallen down.

Pathetic picture  
Of sight and sound,  
If it weren’t for you,  
Who come around.

Not only did you clear the skies,  
You filled our hearts with hope and pride,  
By bringing us Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa and Gaura-Nitāi,  
The paramparā and your priceless smile….

(Written by Gobaṭa dāsa )

## Sao Paulo

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

You are our eternal father. We were simply dead men, but by coming in contact with Your Divine Grace, we recovered our life air, consciousness, and existence as well. Your presence among us used to maintain us. Although you have taught that *vāṇī* association is more important than that of *vapuḥ,* still we know how you used to travel all over this planet, not only to preach, but to tend your spiritual children and keep them away from *māyā.*

But that day arrived and, answering to Lord Kṛṣṇa’s call, you kindly accepted His invitation and went back to the spiritual world. But we had to stay here, without you. Certainly you are still with us in your transcendental instructions, but by thinking that you are simply some kind of energy present in your books or instructions would mean to deny that you are an individual soul engaged now in some ecstatic pastimes in the forest of Vṛndāvana, together with your transcendental friend.

Fortunately, you left with us your address and enlightened to all the way towards your divine presence. You used to call yourself our eternal father, not only a formal *dīkṣā-guru.* We remember when you told Vibhāvatī, Īśāna’s wife: “Actually, I am not your spiritual master. This title is a mere formality. You should consider me as your spiritual father, your *eternal* father.”

But the Māyāvadis do not accept the spiritual master as the eternal father. They would say this is also *māyā.* But *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (3.25.20) states,

*Prasaṅgam ajaraṁ pāśam*  
*ātmanaḥ kavayo viduḥ*  
*sa eva sādhuṣu kṛto*  
*mokśa-dvāram apāvṛtam*

**“Every learned man knows very well that attachment to matter is the greatest entanglement for the spiritual soul. But when this very same attachment goes to the self-realized devotees, this opens up the doors to liberation.”**

Therefore, nobody should be so stupid to the point of not desiring to be attached to you or simply to avoid you.

In *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* you explain how, after Kṛṣṇa’s departure as well as that of their husbands, Draupadi and Subhadrā remained alone. And in your purport you wrote, “The spiritual master, the father, the mother, the relatives, the husband and others, all of them can help during this lifetime, but when one crosses the ocean, he has to take care of himself and use the instructions he had received.” (*Bhāg.* 1.15.50)

Therefore there is no use for us now to cry for help. It is time now that we have to take care of ourselves, by understanding thoroughly your Bhaktivedanta purports and following your instructions in our own lives.

Although the path back to Godhead may be clear, there are many other paths that try to interfere, and they all seem to be “transcendental,” but actually do not lead there. Therefore we simply try to have some intelligence, hoping to reach our final goal, without deviating ourselves by looking to other’s proposals of so-called transcendentalists. We only wish to meet you again so that we can better assist you in the spiritual abode.

*Your eternal servants from Sao Paulo Mandira,*

## Scotland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvati Gosvami. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya-deva and are delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

*mūkaṁ karoti ācālaṁ*  
*paṅguṁ laṅghayate girim*  
*yat-kṛpā tam ahaṁ vande*  
*śrī-guruṁ dīna-tāraṇam*

**“By the mercy of the** ***guru*** **even a dumb man can become the greatest orator, and even a lame man can cross mountains.”**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

We offer our worthless obeisances unto your lotus feet with all the humility and sincerity that we can muster up.

In *Caitanya-caritāmṛta* you wrote in one of your purports that “A spiritual master is recognized as an actual *guru* when it is seen that he has changed the character of his disciples.” But we see very clearly with you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, that you not only changed the character of your thousand of disciples, but you are changing the character of the whole planet by your books; therefore you are actually *jagat-guru.*

By your clear instructions in your Bhaktivedanta purports we are seeing more and more clearly each day—seeing clearly our own position, your greatness, the greatness of your mission, the absolute need for your mission, and just how your books are bringing about a transcendental revolution in the lives of the misdirected fallen souls of this dark, quarrelsome age.

Day by day on book distribution we are meeting souls who are appreciating your books and are buying more from us. This brings joy to our hard hearts. Also, we know that it brings joy to your heart, Śrīla Prabhupāda. We know that you are pleased by book distribution, and that is what motivates and inspires us more and more. We just want to please you; otherwise, life is just like death. We live as servants of yours only to bring pleasure to you.

We are not greatly elevated souls; we are of the lowest parentage and birth, and our previous activities are totally unmentionable. And all we have to offer anyone is only what you have given us. Your mercy is all that we are made of. Please continue without cessation to shower us with your mercy rain, which is extinguishing the fire of material desires within our hearts. By your mercy you have made our otherwise complicated and entangles lives simple and sublime.

You have given us the perfect formula for once again becoming ourselves. You have forced open our darkened eyes, and now that we are becoming a little used to the light, we are liking what we see. You are making everything so clear and obvious by your wonderful purports. Continually we are becoming more and more amazed at just how simply you present this Kṛṣṇa consciousness, making it easily acceptable for everyone, and finding a way by which everyone can serve or be given an opportunity to serve the Lord.

You are most definitely Lord Caitanya’s special mercy upon us fallen, wretched, miserly souls. You are presenting Kṛṣṇa consciousness in such an amazing way. We now see why your *Guru* Mahārāja sent you to the West, because you, the expert, the special mercy of Lord Gaura, are the only one of his disciples who knows how to present Kṛṣṇa consciousness in a universally acceptable way—a way acceptable and understandable to the fallen souls of Kali-yuga.

One to deliver the message  
In this dense dark age,  
Give it to the fallen  
And ask for no wage.

To deliver to all  
In just the right way,  
Fully understandable for the souls  
Of this fallen day.

Then out of Vṛndāvana came,  
Armed with Mahā-mantra,  
Lord Gaura’s secret weapon,  
Our ever well-wisher, A.C. Bhaktivedanta.

When you came, you brought with you  
The most wonderful gift—  
Your translation of Bhāgavatam  
For the fallen to uplift.

You gave us the order  
To distribute your books everywhere,  
From the North to South Pole.  
There’s fallen souls everywhere.

So now let us take up your order  
As our life and soul;  
The distribution of your books  
Our life’s endless goal.

Thank you for your Bhagavatam  
And your Bhagavad-gītā too.  
Thank you for your Kṛṣṇa books  
That reveal our Lord of Blue.

Thank you for all your books;  
They are all full nectar sublime.  
Thank you for the Mahā-mantra  
That frees the fallen of this quarrelsome time.

Thank you Śrīla Prabhupāda!

*Your insignificant servants of the Scottish Yatra,*

## Seattle

You came to Seattle  
In September of ‘68  
And preached to the students.  
You set them straight!

You sanctified so many places  
With your interesting faces.  
T.V. and Radio interviews,  
You came here and gave us the news!

You said not to be attached  
To this or that result,  
Just to please make the endeavour  
To preach Lord Caitanya’s cult.

You asked us to at least maintain  
And not give up the fight,  
Cooperate and read your books  
And everything will be all right.

1988, twenty years since you came,  
And your movement is hardly the same.  
We really haven’t grown that much.  
O Prabhupāda, we so need your touch.

O Śrīla Prabhupāda,  
Please help us maintain  
And to overcome the wane—  
Śrī Caitanya’s moon, waxing again!

O Prabhupāda,  
By your example you show  
How to perfect our lives.  
Develop love for the Lord.

O Gurudeva,  
Please give us the chance  
To finish up here  
And join you, and dance!

All glories to you  
O lord of the true,  
Glories—glories  
To Jagat-guru

(Written by Gaurīdāsa Paṇḍita dāsa)

## Singapore

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances unto your lotus feet. All glories to your transcendental ISKCON. Hare Kṛṣṇa.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, the more we try to engage in your service and the longer we endure, the more we realize your greatness, your magnanimity. You are simply not of this world. You are definitely the topmost devotee of Śrī Kṛṣṇa. You are the ocean of mercy, a quality visible only in Kṛṣṇa and His pure representative like yourself. By these qualities you are shining brightly and never, ever will be surpassed. Out of your kind mercy only, you are time and time again, by your inconceivable potency, strongly guiding us, and we are very confidently sure of this fact; otherwise how is Kṛṣṇa blessing our movement? Without the grace of a bona fide spiritual master as yourself, nothing works, nothing can work. So please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are simply begging you to kindly bestow your mercy unto us here, so that we can somehow or other please you in our humble attempt to participate in your transcendental mission. Kindly do allow us, as we are devoid of any intelligence, humility—in fact, of everything.

Please forgive us, and we hope to remain in your service eternally.

*Your servants,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Sundaragopāla dāsa

## South London

*yeṣāṁ saṁsmaraṇāt puṁsāṁ*  
*sadyaḥ śuddhyanti vai gṛhāḥ*  
*kiṁ punar darśana-sparśa-*  
*pāda-śaucāsanādibhiḥ*

**“One can immediately purify his entire house by remembering such an exalted person as you, to say nothing of directly seeing you, touching your lotus feet, washing your feet, or offering you a sitting place and other worship and service in our home.” (*****Bhāg.*** **1.19.33)**

*My dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to you!

By reading a biography, some letters, or a diary, by hearing tape recordings of a deceased person’s voice, or by the remembrance of those who knew him, it is possible to know a lot about a person. But to get to know him, which necessitates the development of a relationship, is not possible. The soul has gone long since, and all remaining words and deeds are like the crumbling memory of Shelley’s “Ozymandias” ( a supposedly great king of ancient times whose only remains, a proud but broken statue, slowly disappear into lonely desert sands.)

It is not the curiosity of a biographer, a scholar, or a collector that leads me daily to hear your voice (“his master’s voice,” my father-in-law quips upon hearing the sound through the door). Every word you say has eternal significance. Your words do not come from a flickering platform of mental concoction, but from realized Kṛṣṇa conscious thoughts. The other day I heard a man comment that it is better to read the original Sanskrit, because something must inevitably be lost in the translation. You replied that the language is unimportant. It is the thoughts that are important. Reading Sanskrit is not the latchkey of understanding. One must enter into an understanding of the thoughts which provoke the words.

It is an extraordinary fact that you can be known in your *vāṇī* form. Gradually, as a sincere person hears about you, reads your writings, and follows your instructions, he can get to know you. By reciprocation from you in uncountable and wonderful ways, you naturally become the central figure in his life. Even when you were physically present with us, you said, “If you want to know me, read my books.”

It is not possible to know anyone in any other way. What we call close relationships are finished with the disappearance of the person who we do not know. My mother once said that she wanted to be close to me by “getting inside my head.” But such attempts by people to know each other and be close are doomed as long as we do not live in the “free light and the spirit.” What we hold in our arms is but a sample of a lost soul’s diseased consciousness—another earthly shape, constructed from the blueprint of a feverish mind. We do not know each other here.

But you have shown us how to be proper people and have proper relationships—spiritual relationships. You have introduced us to the pivot of all existence, Lord Kṛṣṇa, by whose grace innumerable expansions may relate to each other. Without being Kṛṣṇa consciousness, a person cannot be conscious, know other people, or relate to them. Rather, he hovers in a lonely solipsist’s dream, a shadowy spark cut off from the shining sun. You have changed all this and put everything in order.

Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda. May I always be your alert and faithful dog, my ears pricked up to the slightest sound of my divine master’s voice coming from your home in Vṛndāvana. May I faithfully wait upon you at your door for the rest of my days. May I please you and so finally be admitted in and granted a place at your hearth.

(Written by Rohiṇīnandana dāsa )

*Your servants of the community and congregation of Śrī Śrī Gaura-Nitāi Bhavana, ISKCON, South London, UK.*

## South Trinidad

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvati Gosvami. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya-deva and are delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

On this most auspicious occasion of your Vyāsa-pūjā today, we remember with devotion your great personality, the personification of the ocean of Lord Caitanya’s mercy. Although Lord Caitanya is known as the most merciful and deliverer of the most fallen, the world had no access to this mercy until you came. The souls are meant to enjoy eternal, blissful life in Kṛṣṇa-loka but are now sleeping in the lap of the witch Māyā and experiencing a terrible nightmare of misidentifying with a material body. Had you not come, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because you revealed who the Supreme is, His name, form, qualities, pastimes, and His abode, the destination of all souls. By your personal, intense love for Lord Kṛṣṇa, you taught the world that there is a greater pleasure than sense gratification. We humbly pray that whenever we think of our great, matchless gift, Kṛṣṇa consciousness, we will think of you who gave it to us, and we’ll feel grateful to assist you in saving the fallen souls.

Jaya Śrīla Prabhupāda!

(Written by Rājarṣi dāsa)

*Your servants at Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa Mandira,*

## Soviet Union

Our obeisances are unto you with all respect,  
On this holy day of Vyāsa-pūjā celebration.  
We beg to describe your glorious effect  
Upon people in our present generation.

We offered you a report this day last year  
About the situation of your movement in USSR.  
There is an update which we would like you to hear,  
Concerning how heroic these devotees are.

The Kazan Cathedral, now a museum of atheism,  
Predominating the center of Leningrad.  
In front, Hari-nāma Saṅkīrtana with enthusiasm  
Making the morose people of that city glad.

On the streets of Moscow you could hear the beat  
Of drums and karatalas playing in time.  
For a month and a half it was very sweet,  
Until it was stopped and considered a crime.

In the town of Riga the people are crying  
To find Kṛṣṇa devotees on the streets singing.  
So happy they were to learn the truth about dying  
And the mercy from God that you are bringing.

Some young men from Armenia decided to stay  
In your movement because they were sincere.  
Tasting your mercy they all began to pray  
For the service to you which is most dear.

They printed your books, but felt dissatisfied  
That the quantity was never enough.  
To obtain your mercy it was all justified,  
No matter how the police became rough.

Among them one boy came who did not speak much.  
All he desired was to distribute your books.  
Fulfilling your wishes, he was successful as such,  
But was arrested and put with murderers and crooks.

In prison he was initiated as Śaci-suta das.  
The authorities, cruel with their intimidation,  
Torture and disease, made him die, what a loss!  
Now we are left without his association.

He could have easily been released if he said  
“I don’t believe in Kṛṣṇa,” that’s all.  
To do that, Śacī-suta would rather be dead.  
His freedom didn’t depend on being outside a wall.

With signs and banners unfurled,  
Demonstrating against Soviet harassment,  
Devotees united all over the world.  
The Kremlin felt embarrassment.

Most who were imprisoned are now released,  
And it is remarkable to know  
Their love for you has only increased,  
While suffering in the Siberian snow.

In Moscow the devotees now print  
A magazine, “The Hare Kṛṣṇa Chronicle.”  
To the classless society it is sent,  
Teaching them what is brahmanical.

Indeed, your movement is special,  
And the Soviet chiefs can’t understand  
How to register it, to make it official,  
For there is nothing like it in all the land.

Now there is openness, or “glasnost,”  
We see it as Lord Caitanya’s plan  
For the Soviets to be blessed most,  
Making ecstatic every child, woman, and man.

Śrīla Prabhupāda, your mercy is all we require  
In order for us to continue.  
Your divine instructions, how they inspire  
And kindle our growing faith in you!

## Soweto, South Africa

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our worthless obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. All glories to your Vyāsa-pūjā day.

Even though none of us here in Soweto ever had the privilege of seeing you in your *vapuḥ* form, we are very conscious of the fact that it is your *vāṇī* which keeps us alive in Kṛṣṇa consciousness.

As years go by and Māyādevī conscientiously carries on with her service of testing us, we feel grateful to you, dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, for giving us the necessary weapons to fight her with: your books, *prasadam,* the association of devotees, the beautiful forms of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Rādhānatha and Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurahari, and the chanting of the holy name.

Just like the frog who wanted to become as big as the bull and swelled up so much that he burst, too often we forget our constitutional position—one ten thousandth the size of the tip of a hair—and become puffed up to the point of bursting. By your inconceivable mercy coming down to us through your representative, our false ego becomes deflated somewhat and we gratefully resume our course on the path back to Godhead.

Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda, our life and soul, dearmost posthumous father, grandfather, friend, and ever well-wisher, on this all-auspicious day when, in order to please your eternal companion, the Lord, Śrī Kṛṣṇa, you kindly made your appearance on this planet, we would like to beg a very great favor from you: that you please infuse us with the burning desire to serve you purely and uncompromisingly, without motivation or interruption, until you may be pleased to grant us your personal *darśana,* which will inspire us even more to carry on serving you eternally, in heaven or hell or Vaikuṇṭha.

*Your servants in Soweto,*  
Rūpa-Raghunātha dāsa / Bhakta Alson / Bhakta Reshan / Viśakhā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Maggie / Nitaī dāsa

## St. Louis

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

As the years go by, sometimes, due to our weak condition, we forget your uncompromising instructions. As we listened to a tape the other day, we were reminded of your mood and mission:

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** Just find out what is the venues to push our books, as much as possible.

**Devotee:** Should I concentrate my time for this?

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** Yes, only. This will be your only business. All over the world, see how the books can be pushed….Somehow or other black market, white market, red market. Let him make as soon as there is taste, there will be very good demand….This Chinese policy or Russian policy will not stand. Simply we require to make ourselves strong. As soon as we become stronger than the communist movement, we will finish it.

**Devotee:** Finish everyone?

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** Yes!

**Devotee:** You mean attack them?

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** That I shall tell later, become little strong. We cannot allow them, because Kṛṣṇa’s policy, *paritrānaya sādhūnāṁ vināśāya duṣkṛtām.* You have to take that policy!

**Devotee:** In Vṛndāvana, one day, on a walk, you were saying that Kṛṣṇa’s soldiers will walk all over the world, and they will approach everyone and say, “Do you believe in Kṛṣṇa?” And if they so no, immediately, kill them!

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** Not kill them with weapon, but kill them by *saṅkīrtana.* They are already dead, so physical killing is for very big, big strong men like Mao and Lenin, not common people. They have to be shown mercy. If they allow us to follow our movement, then we are satisfied. And when there is hindrance, the person who is hindering, finished!

**Devotee:** You think the time will come when we will?

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** Just like our Tripurāri, some lady inquired is there any instruction about the power shortage? Oh, yes! So, she purchased, next day she said there is nothing about the power shortage. So you think he is wrong, that is your version?

**Devotee:** I think a simple lie like that is not bad.

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** But a devotee’s role is that whatever may be her inquiry, if she purchases one book, she’ll be benefited. This is the point. But you as big moralist, you think that he has cheated. He has not cheated. He has benefited her. That somehow or other he has given a book to her, someday she’ll read and be benefited. But you people, you think that “Why we should say like that, yes there is some solution of power shortage?” Actually there is, but you think that he has lied. Just like, there is sometimes a child suffering from fever, so the father wants that the child take the medicine and promises him, “If you take the medicine, I’ll give you this cake.” The purpose is the child will take the medicine. That is the purpose. But when the father promises that “If you take the medicine, I’ll give you cake,” cake is dangerous for the child, because he is suffering from fever. But still the father allures him, that “If you take the medicine, and I’ll give the cake.” So, is the father wrong or right?

So similarly, if somehow or other one can sell one book to a person, it is good for him. Don’t think how he’s selling. But he’s giving one book to a person, and he’s getting something, and that’s all. But big moralist, they cannot understand. They will see that: “Why the father has spoken lie to the son? He’s not a good father.” They’re missing. Father is always right. Father cannot be enemy. But for the benefit of the rascal child, sometimes he has to say like that, that “If you take the medicine, I’ll give you the cake.” So, those who are mundane moralist, they cannot understand, because they are mundane platform! The other example is Yudhisṭhira Mahārāja. He was asked by Kṛṣṇa that “You speak lies to Droṇ*ācārya*.” Yudhiṣṭhira Mahārāja refused. For this he had to see hell. He was more moralist than Kṛṣṇa! For this moral activity he had to visit hell.

This philosophy cannot be understood by neophyte devotees. Our purpose is, why we are placing so much stress on books? Because our mission should be preached very widely. That we want! Somehow or other let him purchase a book. That is our mission. There is no question of transgressing moral principles. Arjuna did it. Arjuna was arguing with Kṛṣṇa that “Killing of my relative it is very abominable. Better I beg and maintain myself than kill my relative and become king.” Did he not say? That is ordinary man’s argument. But as Yudhiṣṭhira Mahārāja disapproved to speak lie and Arjuna, in spite of condition, that “It is sinful to kill my relatives,” still he agreed to kill. Why?

**Devotee:** This is supreme moral, Kṛṣṇa’s desire.

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** What Kṛṣṇa desires, that is above these material. But it should not be manufactured! The thing is that our spiritual master wants to preach Kṛṣṇa consciousness, and he has repeatedly said to distribute books and distribute books by any means. That is all! And if you become moralist, that they are taking this means, and that means, for selling the book, “Oh, I am big moralist! I am bigger that Him!”

The spiritual master says that book-selling, there is no question of moral or immoral. Just like in fighting, when there is fight, the soldiers they kill the infantry, there is no question of moral or immoral. They must do it. We want that book-selling must be increased as much as possible. That is what we want. The same principle, the child takes the medicine, never mind the father is speaking lies. Because as soon as you take the medicine, you’ll be benefited. End justifies the means! End is that everyone should have a Kṛṣṇa literature. Doesn’t matter what is the means. Because he has taken one Kṛṣṇa literature, that justifies everything.

So please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us enter into your menial service.

## Sunshine Coast

Let us offer our respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, on this auspicious appearance day. The appearance of Śrīla Prabhupāda marks the beginning of good fortune for a misdirected civilization. A Vaiṣṇava like Śrīla Prabhupāda is certainly a touchstone for all living entities. Whether one is self-realized, a *yogī,* a materialist, or whatever, he will gain all good fortune and satisfaction of the soul by coming in contact with Śrīla Prabhupāda. The association of our *gurudeva* can be compared to the association of the sun planet. By the appearance of the sun, all darkness is dissipated. Similarly, by the appearance of His Divine Grace Śrīla Prabhupāda, ignorance is destroyed and the living entity comes to his constitutional position as part and parcel spirit soul, servant of Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

Transcendental instructions flow from your mouth like honey from a beehive, giving us a taste of real life. We were like dead persons until that honey touched our mouths and ears. Now by the unbounded mercy of Śrīla Prabhupāda a slight greed has risen in our hearts. We wish to taste more of that nectar which flows from Śrīla Prabhupāda’s lotus lips.

So in this holy of all days, we wish to offer our prayers to that great Vaiṣṇava who is everything to us. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are our worshippable spiritual master, our captain, our guiding star, our light in the darkness.

Kindly allow us to continue as your servants, hearing and following your orders. This we would consider your mercy and the perfection of our lives.

*Your servants of the Sunshine Coast.*

## Switzerland

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

**“I offer my respectful obeisances unto His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda, who is very dear to Lord Kṛṣṇa, having taken shelter at His lotus feet. Our respectful obeisances are unto you, O spiritual master, servant of Sarasvati Gosvami. You are kindly preaching the message of Lord Caitanya-deva and are delivering the Western countries, which are filled with impersonalism and voidism.”**

**Prabhupāda Vāṇī and Śikṣā.**

In *Cc. Madya-līlā* 22.115, the following points on the principles of devotional service are stressed.

**One must accept a bona fide spiritual master.**

Actually, in Kali-yuga on one even knows what a genuine spiritual master is -there are so many cheaters. But by great fortune we have come in contact with Śrīla Prabhupāda, and especially with his books. In those books the blind and misguided people of today can find out what a spiritual master actually is.

We have all experienced how the spiritual master appears before us at the proper moment. He appears in the disciplic succession from Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu. He didn’t cheat us by saying “I am God.” Rather, he showed himself to be the servant of God and taught us who God really is, who we are, and how we can go back home, back to Kṛṣṇa. Śrīla Prabhupāda has given us the ticket. For this we are forever in his debt. Basically a disciple simply makes himself look foolish in his attempt to pay back this debt, but still we try to surrender our life, money, intelligence, and words to the glorious mission of Śrīla Prabhupāda.

**One must take initiation from him.**

Spiritual life begins with initiation. One is born for the second time. By the mercy of Śrīla Prabhupāda our infamous life was changed—like rusty iron into gold. Although he had to take up the risk of being criticized by so many envious persons, Śrīla Prabhupāda considered it necessary to strictly follow the instructions of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu and Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Thākura. Therefore he had the power to make thousands (and in the future, by his books, millions and billions) of *mlecchas* and *yavanas* into dancing peacocks.

**One must serve the spiritual master and…**

…**accept instructions from him, ask him questions, and learn the process of devotional service from him.**

Śrīla Prabhupāda once said, “There is actually only one question to ask the spiritual master: “How can I serve you?” All power and intelligence come from his instructions and service to his lotus feet. Śrīla Prabhupāda said at the end, “There is nothing more to say. Everything is in my books. If you want to know me, then read my books.” Śrīla Prabhupāda instructed us to at least maintain what he had given us and to work together to expand Kṛṣṇa consciousness. That is the criterion to show how much we actually love Śrīla Prabhupāda.

Śrīla Prabhupāda has given us everything; now we must become proper followers. The mercy is there as long as we follow his instructions. The instructions of the spiritual master are more important than our own life, more important even than going back home, back to Godhead.

**One must follow in the footsteps of the previous** ***ācārya*****s and follow the guidelines of one’s own spiritual master.**

Śrīla Prabhupāda followed blindly his spiritual master and the *guru-parampara.* In his books he strictly followed the guidelines of the previous *ācāryas,* never giving his own opinion. No, “I think …” No speculation.

Śrīla Prabhupāda is a wandering place of pilgrimage. Wherever he went, he made that place a *tirtha.* He gave us Kṛṣṇa in the form of His holy name, the Deities, *Kṛṣṇa-prasadam,* and his books. He brought light into the darkness. He gave us the vision to see what is good and what is bad. Actually he told us, “Everything here is bad—only Kṛṣṇa and those things which are connected to Him are good.” Because we now can have the association of Vaiṣṇava, we can give up bad association. And we now have Śrīla Prabhupāda’s mission. What more worthwhile thing is there in the three worlds? Śrīla Prabhupāda gave us *Bhagavad-gītā, Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam, Caitanya-caritāmṛta, The Nectar of Devotion,*and *Īsopaniṣad*— enough books to immerse ourselves fully in the nectar of Kṛṣṇa consciousness. And we should simply distribute these books throughout the entire world. That will especially satisfy Śrīla Prabhupāda, the *ācāryas,* and Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, let us continue to dance in ecstasy and distribute the glories of Kṛṣṇa and His devotees throughout the world.

All glories to Śrīla Prabhupāda!

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s transcendental book distribution *kī jaya!*

(written by Rohiṇī-suta dāsa)

*Your servants at Gokula-dhāma, Switzerland,*

## Sydney

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

To our divine master, Śrīla Prabhupāda,

On this all-auspicious day of your divine appearance we are attempting to express our love for you. Naturally this is an impossible task. We, as your disciples and granddisciples, can never repay our debt to you, and offering worthy praise to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is like holding a candle to the sun.

A direct emissary from the supramundane realm, the spiritual world, you have blessed mother Bhūmi by the touch of your lotus feet and crushed the Māyāvadis and materialistic rascals by the power of your knowledge and unshakeable faith. In *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam,*the symptoms of a *sadhu* are enunciated as:

*titikṣavaḥ kāruṇikāḥ*  
*suhṛdaḥ sarva-dehinām*  
*ajāta-śatravaḥ śāntāḥ*  
*sādhavaḥ sādhu-bhūṣaṇāḥ*  
*SB 3.25.21*

**“He is tolerant, merciful, and friendly to all living entities. He has no enemies, he is peaceful, he abides by the scriptures, and all his characteristics are sublime.”**

These jewellike words of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* are much more fitting to decorate the gold of your personality, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Your tolerance is as deep as the ocean, O divine master, forever apparent before us. You tolerated the austerity of traveling so far across the hazardous seas, leaving the spiritual world of Vṛndāvana to fulfill the instruction of your *Guru* Mahārāja. You tolerated with patience the hellish conditions you met in that tenth-class Western sphere and forgave us our offenses unknowingly committed at your lotus feet.

Just as your tolerance is as deep as the mighty ocean, Your Divine Grace’s mercy is as vast. How kind and merciful you were to us. You gifted us and humanity with your erudite, empowered learning in the wonderful form of your books—“the lawbooks for the next ten thousand years”; the conclusions of the *Vedanta* so skillfully explained that even fools such as we can apply them in our everyday lives.

By your genuine concern and compassion for the conditioned souls, people from all walks of life found you so wonderful and magnetic. Even the belligerent realized your greatness and how fallen they were for being so ill-mannered in your presence. All but the completely blind became conscious of at least a glimpse of your greatly exalted stature as *sevaka-bhagavān,* the servitor Personality of Godhead.

Because you are a completely self-realized and surrendered servant of Lord, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are so peaceful as the ocean, which remains unperturbed as the rivers enter its waters. Sitting in your divine presence even for a second removed all anxieties, fears, and apprehensions that torture the conditioned soul.

You are the personification of the Vedic injunctions coupled with the mercy of the *Mahā-bhāgavata.* You have taught us everything, from how to eat and bathe, to worshipping the Deities according to the Pañcarātrika-vidhi.

The last quality described in the verse is “all his characteristics are sublime.” This is the perfect description of Your Divine Grace. No fault can blemish your divine character; no tinge of material influence mars your transcendental personality. Even the demigods proffer their *praṇāmas* at your lotus feet. Out of His causeless mercy, Śrī Kṛṣṇa directly ordered you to walk among us—to pick us up from the quagmire of sinful desires and place us again in our constitutional position.

Beloved father, divine master, our hearts are broken from our separation from you. We beg you to please place us eternally as particles of the dust of your lotus feet, birth after birth.

(Written by Kṛṣṇarūpa-devī dāsī)

*The devotees of Sydney Temple,*

## Toronto

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*  
Please accept our humble obeisances.

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

On this most auspicious day, we pray to Lord Kṛṣṇa that we may more clearly see how to apply the perfectly clear instructions given by you.

We pray to you to continue cleansing our consciousness of all inauspicious thoughts which separate us from our original state of pure Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Please continue to pray to Lord Kṛṣṇa for our protection and advancement. We are dependent upon you as little children are dependent on their grown-up parents. We hope that we never become so “mature” that we feel no longer dependent on you.

You are our life and soul. As Śrīla Visvanath Cakravarti Thākura prays, “When the spiritual master is pleased, Kṛṣṇa is pleased. And when he is not pleased, Kṛṣṇa is not pleased.” We feel successful only if we can perform some service which increases the smile on your lotus face.

As we reflect upon our present circumstances, we count our blessings. We shudder to think what our lives might have been like without your precious association.

As one blind man following another ended up in a ditch, we blindly chased after our self-interest (which was actually our sense interest) without knowing what the self is. While we very expertly cared for the cage, the bird inside starved—until you entered our lives.

As a drowning man feels forever obliged to the person who rescued him from his perilous condition, we all feel eternally indebted to you.

You gave us transcendental knowledge:

This is eternal; this is temporary.  
This is real; this is illusion.  
This is favorable; this is not.

Kṛṣṇa is absolute. He is nondifferent from His holy name…Tirelessly you spoke and wrote. Selflessly you gave yourself to us and the world.

We know that we have acted in ways which disappointed you, but please forgive us and continue to correct us, and please don’t ever kick us away. Thank you, Śrīla Prabhupāda.

(Written by Girijādava dāsa)

*Your insignificant servants of New Remunā Dhāma.*

## Udhampur

*Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine feet. All glories to Śrī *Guru* and Gaurāṅga. All glories to Your Divine Grace.

Your Divine Grace showed the way to live a peaceful and God conscious life to the whole world. Your Divinity gave us a home which can accommodate the whole world. We are indebted to you for your eternal mercy and blessings showered upon us by giving this *Mahā-mantra,* Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rama, Hare Rama, Rama Rama, Hare Hare. We always feel your transcendental presence while going through your valuable books. Your Divine Grace is the true representative of Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa for having the same sympathy and love for the fallen conditioned souls like us as Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa has, in sending true representatives like yourself from time to time to save us from this material, miserable world of sufferings.

So far as reality is concerned, we are not in a position to glorify your real contribution rendered to the humanity as a whole. As one cannot explain the depth and the width of the sea, in the same way we are unable to express Your Divine Grace’s real position.

Whatever offenses we have made while paying our offerings to your lotus feet, we beg for your forgiveness. Please give us strength to carry on your mission, as well as to run the Śrīla Prabhupāda Āśrama nicely. We, being fallen, conditioned souls, commit mistakes time and again. Please guide us from time to time.

We pay our humble obeisances at your lotus feet.

*Your eternal servants.*

## Uppsala, Sweden

*nama oṁ viṣṇu-pādāya kṛṣṇa-preṣṭhāya bhū-tale*  
*śrīmate bhaktivedānta-svāmin iti nāmine*

*namas te sārasvate deve gaura-vāṇī-pracāriṇe*  
*nirviśeṣa-śūnyavādi-pāścātya-deśa-tāriṇe*

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your lotus feet!

We have now been here preaching in Uppsala, the biggest university town in Sweden, for one year. Actually, Śrīla Prabhupāda, you came here in 1973 and also gave a lecture at the university.

At that time there was a lot of communist ideas going around at the universities in Sweden, so when you started to talk about first-, second- , third-, and fourth-class men in your lecture, there was a violent reaction. Some students were standing up screaming, pointing at you, accusing you: “..and you are of course a first-class man!” in a very challenging way. But you were not at all disturbed and answered in a totally humble and surrendered way that you looked upon yourself as a “fifth-class man serving all the other four classes”!

Then at the end of the lecture there was a heavy discussion about God and “Who is God.” One man was especially agitated, standing up in his chair speaking with a loud voice. You were discussing with him for some time, and you finally got him to admit that he actually did not know who is God. So then you ended the discussion very, very forcefully, by saying loudly, “If you do not know who is God, then you take it from me, and I say that KṚṢṆA IS GOD!” That completely silenced everybody, and some were starting to applaud you!

So in this way, you very intelligently and forcefully established the supremacy of Kṛṣṇa consciousness amongst the Swedish atheistic university students here in 1973 /

Now it’s 1988, and we have also been at the university lecturing a few times, and some teachers there still remember you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, giving that strong lecture, smashing the atheists and establishing Kṛṣṇa consciousness as real reality!

All glories, all glories to you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, savior of everybody!

(Written by Prapūjaka dāsa)

**Disciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Prapūjaka dāsa

**Granddisciple of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Śikaṇḍinī-devī dāsī

## Vancouver

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances. We feel completely inadequate in our meager attempts to glorify your unlimited, exalted transcendental qualities. Being enmeshed on the mundane platform, we just cannot comprehend the oceanic extent of your *acintya-śakti. Acintya-śakti*—what else can we call it?

You have made such an astounding impact on this world, an impact so profound that it melted the coldest, hardest steel-framed hearts. Westerners were made into Vaiṣṇava. The impact of your *acintya-śakti* is so far reaching, so massive, that the roots of Vaisnavism are now firmly established on every continent. Once enveloped in the darkest ignorance, this world has been illuminated by the radiant impact of your preaching. You made Kṛṣṇa consciousness understandable to everyone: children, laymen, theologists, scientists. It’s awesome. Meaning and fulfillment has been brought to aimless frustrated lives, unity to a culture of mistrust, happiness to a world of misery. You brought the *prasādam* “weapon” that has irrevocably changed countless lives. You freely distributed the most valuable thing, the *cintāmaṇī* stone of the *mahā-mantra,* from your unlimited treasure house of love of God. The books you wrote have changed the lives of thousands and will be the guiding light for thousands of years to come. Temples of Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa have been established throughout the world by your divine mercy. You have established centers of Vaiṣṇava culture and association. Ratha-yātra has been introduced in so many cities.

You have inexplicably changed the face of the world and saved the lives of countless souls.

How can it be? How have you made this impact, this “Hare Krishna Explosion”?

*Acintya-śakti.* Can it be otherwise? There is no doubt that you are *śakty-āveśa,* a directly empowered representative of the Supreme Personality of Godhead, Lord Śrī Kṛṣṇa.

In your absence we are like small children trying to walk in the footsteps of a giant. No one can imitate your commanding stature. This we have seen. There is none to replace you. You are the founder-*ācārya,* our eternal guide and shelter.

You have left us with the heavy responsibility of maintaining and expanding your colossal achievements. This is a great anxiety for us, as we are so totally insignificant in light of your greatness.

We humbly fall at your lotus feet and beg that if we have rendered you any favorable service in our crores of births in the material world, please bless us with the intelligence and humility to cooperate together to carry on your mission. You said this would be the test of our love for you. Our hearts are steel-framed and cold as ice, but we are confident that if we can somehow do this (cooperate together to carry on your mission), Lord Kṛṣṇa will surely allow us to develop affection for you, and He will surely give us the ability to render some minute service in your divine mission.

*Your servants in Vancouver*

## Victoria, Australia

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept my humble obeisances.

I would like to offer to you this poem in appreciation for all that you have done for me since I first came in contact with ISKCON in 1975.

’75 alive, alive.  
I’m 21; my life just begun.  
The future is ripe for a sensual life,  
Nothing special, mind you, just a car and a wife.

But let’s look at it now, this future of mine.  
Is it all that rosy, is it all that fine,  
A house in the suburbs, a wife and a car,  
My journey traversed only come this far?

But what are the options? What else can I do?  
Take drugs to be happy? Go back to school?  
Live in a commune and do my own thing?  
It all seems so boring, so utterly frustrating.

My mind is revolting, not wanting to follow.  
My heart’s vacant—no love, just hollow.  
There’s something amiss, not just how to have fun.  
I’m 21, but my life’s not begun.

When I read that first book with tears in my eyes  
And I looked at the devotees with genuine smiles  
I wanted to join them, to live with them then.  
The power of this world was matched by your pen.

It’s 13 years on now since that momentous day,  
And all I can do is continually pray  
That your light will shine on me forever more.  
Please hold on to me; don’t let me fall.

I don’t really deserve it; it’s not a demand.  
If you choose not to love me, I could understand.  
But the truth is, I’ve no one; you are everyone;  
And I am your ward, your wayward son.

You have given me life, hope, and good will.  
I’ll try to pass on the knowledge that fills  
Your books and your lectures that you left behind  
When you went to Kṛṣṇa, our Lord sublime.

We have recently begun a new farm project for you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, and all of us here at New Nanda Grāma would like you to know that we will try our very best to represent you well and be good devotees in ourselves.

(Written by Krodheśvara dāsa)

*Your aspiring servants,*

**Disciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Krodheśvara dāsa / Vasuśreṣṭha dāsa

**Granddisciples of Śrīla Prabhupāda**

Gokula dāsa / Mānmohana dāsa / Caraṇāmṛta-devī dāsī / Bhakta Ian / Śuklam

## Walla, Walla, Washington

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances in the dust of your lotus feet. Walla Walla, Washington, is a new name in this year’s Vyāsa-pūjā book. Five devotees are chanting sixteen rounds regularly, and two devotees were recently initiated. In days when many temples are struggling, we wanted to offer our little preaching center to your service. Like Walla Walla, there are many conditioned souls out there in the material world waiting to hear about the incredible mercy of Śrīla Prabhupāda is His authorized representative. Kṛṣṇa wants all the conditioned souls to end their suffering and return back home, back to Godhead. In Walla Walla, Washington, the devotees have been reading your books, and by your incredible mercy they started chanting and adopting the four regulative principles. We offer this preaching center, therefore, to you on this most auspicious occasion of your appearance day celebration. Jaya Prabhupāda!

(Offering prepared by Vipramukhya Swami on behalf of the devotees in Walla Walla)

Janārdana dāsa / Lakṣmī-priyā-devī dāsī / Bhaktin Lucy / Bhakta Trinidad / Bhakta Thomas / Joshua

## Washington, D.C.

All glories to His Divine Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami, Śrīla Prabhupāda, founder-*ācārya* of the International Society for Krishna Consciousness, the rising wave of Lord Caitanya’s ocean of mercy to save all living entities.

The energy with which you moved this society into Vedic history is transforming the darkest regions of our hearts and this earth planet into the spring garden of the Gauḍīya Vaiṣṇava. Due to the mercy of the Mahā-mantra, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Hare Kṛṣṇa, Kṛṣṇa Kṛṣṇa, Hare Hare / Hare Rama, Hare Rama, Rama Rama, Hare Hare, this garden will flourish for thousands of years. By your grace, the experience of rediscovering our love for Lord Kṛṣṇa weeds out all misgivings, producing a joy exceeding all expectations. The soothing effect of your eternal presence and ever-fresh words causes our appreciation for your compassion and purity to blossom like fragrant new lotus flowers. Śrīla Prabhupāda, we pray that as our ever well-wisher, you will continue to shower us with your blessings so that we may always ride the eternal tidewaters of Lord Caitanya’s mercy by serving in the dust of your lotus feet.

Śrīla Prabhupāda-kī Jaya!

(Written by Haladhara Dāsa)
