## Vyāsa-pūjā Homages from the Publishers

## North American BBT

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. We pray that we may humbly serve them eternally.

By your grace, our life has been made very simple if we are willing to just follow your instructions as they are. All answers are there; we must just look to them with absolute faith. You told one devotee, “Book distribution must be given stress always. He has spoken the right thing. If you do this sincerely, it is a fact that Kṛṣṇa will supply everything else required. I blindly follow my *Guru* Mahārāja. I do not know what is the result.” Only because you had this absolute blind faith in the order of your *Guru* Mahārāja were you the only one who came to this most fallen part of the planet to deliver us from a hellish life of bondage. Therefore I would like to remember some of the personal instructions that you left for us, both through letters and purports.

You knew that as long as we were here in this material body, and still very much attached to it, we would be concerned with some economic arrangements. So you combined and linked the preaching with this book-selling. “In this way, if we can work on our publications, all our poverty shall immediately be subdued.”

Then you revealed your plot in anther letter: “If you simply push on this one activity of distributing my books, your all success will be there. I have hatched this ‘transcendental plot’ for getting money by selling my books, and if we stick only to this plan and use our brain only for selling books, there will easily be enough money.” Everyone is in so much anxiety about how to solve these problems, but we have become poverty-stricken because we deviated from your ‘transcendental plot.’ If we look around the world, it is easy to see that those who have stuck with it despite all obstacles are now prospering. On the other hand, those who have strayed from the path are plagued with so many problems. You have explained in the *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* (7.13.32) further how these problems are solved?

“The *brahmana* continued: I am actually seeing how a rich man, who is a victim of his senses, is very greedy to accumulate wealth, and therefore suffers from insomnia due to fear from all sides, despite his wealth and opulence.”

In the later part of the purport you state, “As far as our Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement is concerned, we are getting money naturally, by the grace of God, by selling our literatures. This literature is not sold for our sense gratification; to spread this Kṛṣṇa consciousness movement we need so many things, and Kṛṣṇa is therefore supplying us the requisite money to advance this mission.”

We pray that now in America, where you first started your BBT, we can have this firm faith in your clear instructions and stick to them in these troubled times as our life-line. You took this as your order from your *Guru* Mahārāja: “This book publication was the most important work of my *Guru* Mahārāja, and he ordered me to continue it in the Western world. So I am very much indebted to all of you who are helping me to carry out the order of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhanta Sarasvati Gosvami.”

Having taken the instructions of your *Guru* Mahārāja as your life and soul, you were always referring to them. So in the same way, our only safe path is to take your order as our life and soul. This is the secure position of following the *paramparā* as Kṛṣṇa instructed Arjuna. If we can just try to put aside our false ego and painstakingly follow your instructions with the same faith with which you followed your *Guru* Mahārāja’s instructions, then our life will be sublime and successful. “As you know, my *Guru* Mahārja emphasized book publication above all other preaching activities because it is the big *mṛdaṅga,* which can be heard all over the world.” So when we give up all other formulas for security and rekindle the missionary preaching mood, we will find that the same nectar in selling your books is still there, just as it always was and will be for the next ten thousand years. Please, Śrīla Prabhupāda, give us your blessings that we may continue to serve you in this way throughout the rest of our lives, always tasting the nectar of your kind association.

Śrīla Prabhupāda’s transcendental book distribution *kī jaya!*

*Your servants of the North American BBT*

## Vyāsa-pūjā Homages from Other Sources

## BTG

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances.

In celebrating your appearance day, we offer you eleven more issues of your magazine, *Back to Godhead,* hoping you will be pleased with our work and with the contributions of many other disciples and followers.

We hope we have not offended you with our wholehearted but meager efforts to deliver your Bhaktivedanta purports to this small and beleaguered planet.

And we hope we have not offended your followers. We have been put into positions as editors and caretakers of your magazine, which means that we have to choose from among the articles submitted, accepting some and rejecting others. And those that we accept sometimes need editing and correction.

Even when we approach this task with great care, our hardworking contributors sometimes take offense. And sometimes we have been careless.

So although this is your Vyāsa-pūjā day, and we should be giving rather than requesting, we beg for your mercy in dealing with these delicate situations.

We are sorry to report that BTG circulation dropped to a record low this year, with most centers decreasing their orders. But the bright news is that the English temples have just recently increased their order to seventy-five thousand a month, accounting for 60% of current BTG circulation. Rule Britannia!

We have firm faith that however ugly and overpowering this Kali-yuga may appear, your teachings have the potency to burn all the ugliness to ashes in short order. We shall therefore keep this BTG flame burning so that it may help initiate the joyous conflagration that is going to consume the unhappiness of this dark age.

*Your servants at BTG*

## Bhaktivedanta Institute

*Our dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our humble obeisances at your divine lotus feet. Our only hope is to develop love for Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa by your mercy of Śrī Śrī Nitāi-Gaurāṅga.

Cultivation of knowledge by the process of scholastic research, experimental evidence, or even cultivation of mystic powers by the yogic processes is not enough. By approaching Kṛṣṇa, knowledge and magical powers can be cultivated; but this is not the real benefit one should seek by *bhakti-yoga.* By *bhakti-yoga,* by your mercy, one should try and re-awaken his love for Śrīmati Rādhārani and Śrī Kṛṣṇa. It is only love for Kṛṣṇa that is satisfying.

We pray that we will always be inspired to read your books with the exclusive aim of getting proper advice on how to proceed in the performance of the glorious activities of devotional service, only and purely for the purpose of getting and giving pure love of Kṛṣṇa.

We want to go back to Godhead, to be engaged in your pastimes of service to Kṛṣṇa, in an ever helpful fashion when we are allowed to meet and be with you.

*Your fallen servants at the Bhaktivedanta Institute (San Francisco).*

## ISKCON World Review

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our most humble obeisances at your lotus feet. All glories to Your Divine Grace on this auspicious occasion of your appearance in this material world.

Currently there are more than three hundred ISKCON temples around the world, and we are sending one copy of the *ISKCON World Review* to every center. We hope that this pleases you. Stories and letters come from devotees all over the world every day.

One aspect of our services is to provide the members of ISKCON with a forum of communication. There have been plenty of controversy in the past decade since your departure, and we hope we have done something positive to help bring about a resolution in some areas.

We want to put Kṛṣṇa in the center by printing the news of the *saṅkīrtana* movement as it spreads to every town and village. We also provide a forum for communication about certain “controversial points” that are being discussed throughout the movement.

On July 28, 1969, you wrote to one of your disciples:

“So far as your question about controversy amongst the disciples of Bhakti Siddhanta Saraswati Goswami Mahārāja, that is a fact. But this controversy is not material. Just like in a national program, different political parties are sometimes in conflict and make propaganda against each other, but their central points is always service to their country. Similarly, amongst the disciples of Bhakti Siddhanta Saraswati there may be some controversy, but the central point is how to preach the mission of His Divine Grace. If the central point is fixed up, then there is no harm in such controversy. Every individual being must have his opinion; that is the significance of individuality, but all such differences of opinions must coincide in Krishna.”

We are very grateful to be part of ISKCON, the house you built. Thank you for accepting us and giving us shelter, despite our numberless shortcomings. We are indebted to you to the core of our hearts and pray for your eternal guidance and protection.

*Your humble servants,*

Mukunda Goswami / Uddhava dāsa / Nandinī-devī dāsī / Drutakarmā dāsa / Jatāyu dāsa / Rakṣaṇa dāsa / Dravidā dāsa / Rādhaā-sevā-devī dāsī / Bill Muster

## Ministry of Vaishnava Cultural History

*Dear Śrīla Prabhupāda,*

Please accept our obeisances. All glory to you and your sacred mission of spreading the teachings of Śrī Caitanya Mahāprabhu.

As children were once souls searching for parents in order to take another body and come into this world, similarly, desirous of gaining spiritual bodies, we chose you, by the grace of the Supreme, to be our spiritual parent. We are fortunate to have taken birth in your spiritual family, Śrī Caitanya’s *saṅkīrtana* movement. How did we qualify to take our second births in such a great tradition?

Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are your eternal children. Children love to present gifts to the parent, especially on the birthday of the parent. As your spiritual children, we love to give you gifts. Indeed, we would like to offer our every action to you and your mission. On your appearance day, however, we acutely feel our own inadequacies in presenting you with any gift whatsoever. We just feel how much we are your helpless children. Anything that we children can offer you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, is simply what you have already offered to us—the nectar of preaching Kṛṣṇa consciousness. We are trying to take up the preaching mission you established. Will you accept our love and affection for you through these efforts?

Children have a natural fascination with every move of the parent, and so we also loved to watch your every action and expression. We were in awe of you, Śrīla Prabhupāda, because everything you did was so intimately and intensely related to Kṛṣṇa. As the behavior of a parent influences the child, your behavior as a preacher was exemplary for us as well as a source of great fascination and joy. However, children tend to imitate the parent’s behavior without understanding the underlying meaning of the action, and so have we been prone to imitating your great personality and behavior. As spiritual infants this is understandable, but as older children we must learn to distinguish between imitation and following your instructions. Will you forgive us for artificiality imitating your greatness and give us another chance to carry out your instructions?

We are just like children, Śrīla Prabhupāda. Between children unnecessary conflicts frequently arise due to false conceptions, such as “Older children are better than younger children,” “Brothers are better than sisters,” and “This can’t be shared with anyone else.” However, when children join together to try to please the parent, there is harmony in the family. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you wanted us to cooperate with each other in love and trust. When will we be able to be honest with our brothers and sisters and resolve these conflicts by striving for the deeper understanding of the tradition that you have passed on to us?

As children are born with qualities from previous births, so we have brought the conditioning of our previous experiences in the material world to our present lives in Kṛṣṇa consciousness. Thus, we have at times misused and misrepresented Gauḍīya philosophy. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you presented the most confidential and sophisticated knowledge of the divinity. The highest philosophy of personalism teaches that God is always a *relation,* never just an object. God is not a *He,* but a *They:* Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa. We worship Śrī Śrī Rādhā-Kṛṣṇa together as the highest and most supreme, and we also worship Śrī Kṛṣṇa Caitanya, who directly appears as that divine love which flows between Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. Divine relationships are therefore at he heart of our theology, and there is no higher understanding than that expressed in the worship of this divine relation of Śrī Śrī Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa. Together They are the very center of our lives. When will we understand and appreciate all of the transcendental relationships that you have given us: our relationships with you, our spiritual master; our relationship with the devotees and our Vaiṣṇava lineage: and our relationship with the Supreme Lord, His sacred names, places, and literature? When will we be cleansed of the conditionings coming from our previous lives and be able to appreciate all these transcendental relationships?

We are just like children, Śrīla Prabhupāda. The relationship between the parent and child is the most important relationship in the child’s life. Because of this, children do not like to be separated from the parent. Behind their work and their play together, they feel emptiness and long to be reunited with the parent. Since you are our spiritual parent, our relationship with you is more important than any relationship that we have had with any parent through innumerable births. Śrīla Prabhupāda, you once left us. Do you know how much we miss you?

In this Ministry’s community of devotees, Śrīla Prabhupāda, we are your children who live together in a tiny part of your big house. Here we collaborate on educational and cultural projects, designed to serve your mission. As we have been working very hard together to serve you, have you seen how much we feel your direct presence in our love and trust for one another? Śrīla Prabhupāda, you are certainly still with us.

We will always be your children, indebted to you for the loving care with which you have raised us and for the rich inheritance you have left to us. From you, the great cultural and historical legacy of Vaisnavism has been inherited by us, and we pray that we may someday utilize this inheritance to assist you in your mission. We in the Ministry humble submit ourselves to you and beg you to accept our various services: researching and writing the history of Vaisnavism; developing higher educational materials and methods for ISKCON; building the “Matsya” library of Vaiṣṇava literature and source materials; writing, translating, and publishing books and journals of high scholarly quality; and designing cultural exhibitions demonstrating the glory of Vaiṣṇava history and thought.

Now we must mature and reciprocate with you by devoting ourselves even more to your mission. Have we even realized what all our responsibilities are?

(Written by Garuḍa dāsa)

*Your dependent children,*

## New Jaipur

But I took that order of my spiritual master very seriously, but I did not think that I’ll have to do such and such thing. I was at that time a householder. This is the arrangement of Kṛṣṇa. If we strictly try to serve the spiritual master, his order, then Kṛṣṇa will give us all facilities. This is the secret. Although there was no possibility, I never thought…But I took it a little seriously by studying a commentary by Viṣvanātha Cakravartī Thākura on the *Bhagavad-gītā.* In the *Bhagavad-gītā,* in connection with one verse: *vyavasāyātmikā buddhir ekeha kuru-nandana,*Viṣvanātha Cakravartī Thākura gives his commentary that we should take up the words from the spiritual master as our life and soul. We should try to carry out the instruction, the specific instruction of the spiritual master very rigidly without caring for our personal benefit or loss. (Śrīla Prabhupāda from a lecture on the Disappearance Day of Śrīla Bhaktisiddhānta Sarasvatī Thākura, December 9, 1968, in Los Angeles, California.)

Śrīla Prabhupāda, you instructed us about the importance of establishing *daiva-varṇāśrama-dharma* beginning especially in 1974 with your now-famous *varṇāśrama* morning walks…

The *varṇāśrama* college has to be established immediately. Everywhere, wherever we have got our centers, a *varṇāśrama* college should be established to train all divisions: one class *brāhmaṇa,* one class *kṣatriya,* one class *vaiśya,* one class *śūdra.* But everyone will be elevated to the spiritual platform by the spiritual activities which we have prescribed.

For the management of affairs we are required to divide because there are different classes of brains. So those who have very intellectual brains, they should become *brāhmaṇas.* Those who are fit for management and protection, they should be trained as *kṣatriyas.* Those who are fit for producing food and taking care of cows, they should be trained as *vaiśyas.* And the balance, they are all *śūdras.*

So in our society these divisions should be there. The most intelligent class of men should be engaged in preaching, reading books, instructing, and taking care of the Deity worship in the temple. Another class will be strong managers to see that everything is going nicely and everyone is engaged. Not that only eating and sleeping. Everyone must be engaged, employed. So if one is very much adapted for eating and sleeping, he should be engaged with plows.

Everyone should be trained as Vaiṣṇava and at the same time he should work in different positions for management.

But if we show that you are actually doing something ideal, then they will appreciate. Make a small community showing ideal life, not idle life.

You become trained up and revise the whole religion of the Western civilization, especially in America. Then a new chapter will come in. This is the program. Therefore *varṇāśrama* school is required.

**Hridayānanda Goswami:** When we first go to open a temple in a city we first get an apartment or a store front. But as more and more people come we should get land and cows, everything, and turn it into a society.

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** Yes. Yes. Yes.

This Hare Kṛṣṇa movement will practically attract the people. If the world affairs are adjusted according to our Kṛṣṇa conscious plan there will be no difficulty for all the nations. All the countries, they will be happy. So we must educate people gradually and by our living example we’ll attract them.

….throughout your purports of *Śrīmad-Bhāgavatam* thereafter….

The conclusion is that is if one is serious about liberation, he not only should execute the duties of *varṇāśrama-dharma* but should also engage in *bhakti-yoga* by beginning hearing from a realized soul. (*Bhāg.* 4.24.53purport)

Because there is no institution to teach people how to become *brāhmaṇa, kṣatriya, vaiśya śūdra* or *brahmacārī, gṛhastha, vānaprastha* and *sannyāsa* these demons want a classless society. This is resulting in chaotic conditions….the Kṛṣṇa Consciousness movement, however, is being propagated all over the world to reestablish the *varṇāśrama-dharma* system and thus save human society from gliding down to hellish life.(*Bhāg.* 5.19.19)

For peace and happiness….the *varṇāśrama* institution must be introduced. The symptoms of one’s activities must be defined, and one must be educated accordingly. Then spiritual advancement will automatically be possible. (*Bhāg.* 7.11.25)

…the mentality of service is called the eternal occupational duty. This eternal occupational duty can be organized through the instruction of varṇāśrama. (*Bhāg.* 7.11.3*)*

In human society, therefore the brahminical culture, the *kṣatriya* culture and *vaiśya* culture must be maintained, and people must be taught how to be satisfied with only what they need. In modern civilization there is no such education; everyone tries to possess more and more, and everyone is dissatisfied and unhappy. The Kṛṣṇa Consciousness movement is therefore establishing various farms, especially in America, to show how to be happy and content with minimum necessities of life and save time for self-realization. (*Bhāg.* 8.19.21)

…if the process of *varṇācrama-dharma* is introduced, even in this age of Kali, the situation of Satya-yuga can be invoked. The Hare Kṛṣṇa movement, or Kṛṣṇa Consciousness movement, is meant for this purpose. (*Bhāg.* 9.10.51)

….in a profoundly important conversation in February of 1977….

**Satsvarūpa Goswami:** Lord Caitanya… when Rāmānanda Rāya brought this up, He said it was not possible in this age to introduce this.

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** Yes. Not…He did not say possible. *Iha bhaya.* Caitanya Mahāprabhu was interested only on the spiritual platform. He had no idea of material side. He rejected material side.

**Satsvarūpa Goswami:** But don’t we do that also?

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** No. Our position is different. We are trying to implement Kṛṣṇa Consciousness in everything. And Caitanya Mahāprabhu personally took *sannyāsa.* He rejected everything material. *Niṣkiñcana.* But we are not going to be *niṣkiñcana.* We are trying to cement the troubled position of the … That is also the prescription of *Bhagavad-gītā.* We are not rejecting the whole society…our duty is that we shall arrange the external affairs also so nicely that one day they will come to the spiritual platform very easily. Paving the way. And Caitanya Mahāprabhu, personality like that, they have nothing to do with the material world. But we are preaching. We are preaching. Therefore, we must pave the situation in such a way that gradually they will be promoted to the spiritual plane…

Therefore *varṇāśrama-dharma* is required, simply show-bottle will not do. So the *vaṛṛāśrama-dharma* should be introduced all over the world, and …

**Satsvarūpa Goswami:** Introducing starting with ISKCON community?

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** Yes. Yes. *Brāhmaṇa, kṣatriyas.* There must be regular education.

**…**.and in the last months before you left the planet when you desired to come to the U.S. just to introduce *vaṛṇāśrama* for the benefit of your disciples and the rest of the denizens suffering in the grip of Kali-yuga’s evil sway…

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** I want to live a little longer to make everything more perfect.

**Tamal Kṛṣṇa Goswami:** Will you do this by enthusing the devotees by staying with them or is there a specific program?

**Śrīla Prabhupāda:** A specific program. I want to introduce *vaṛṇāśrama.* At our Pennsylvania farm, the biggest problem of life is solved: food.

We are nurturing a desire to fulfill your wishes, understanding that to execute your order gives our lives meaning.

Therefore, by recourse to your timeless purports, as well as the *Mahābharata, Rāmāyaṇa, Manu-saṁhitā,* and other authorized Vedic literatures, we now vow to attempt to recreate a model community founded on the principles of Vedic civilization, as well as to establish an educational institution for training others. We vow to combine the practice of *bhakti-yoga* with the divine social order established by the Supreme Personality of Godhead Himself to insure smooth progress in Kṛṣṇa Consciousness and to establish a visible and viable shelter for humanity at large and ourselves. As you have pointed out, to preach and ideal civilization one must live it. Please bless us, inspire us and direct us in the fulfillment of your supremely compassionate plans for the welfare of the world. May we ever remain instrumental as dust particles at your lotus feet.

*Your servants at New Jaipur,*

Nityānanda dāsa / Rūpa Vilasā dāsa / Jñānagamya dāsa / Bhakti Vimal / Kanyā Kumārī-devī dāsī / Chandrika-devī dāsī / Mahāmāyā-devī dāsī / Kāmāri-kāntā-devī dāsī / and all the children.

## Tridaṇḍī Bhikṣu B.H. Mangal

On Thy Holy Advent, O Swami Jagad-*guru*!

The Vaiṣṇava is the only *guru* of the three worlds. The Vaiṣṇava is *śikṣā-guru,* and *jagad-guru* as far as different kinds of relationships are concerned. When the Vaiṣṇava accepts disciples, out of his causeless mercy, he is called *dīkṣā-guru.* When he teaches in general on the same subject of *Viṣṇu-bhakti,* he is called *śikśā-guru*. He may also be within himself without concern for this world. In all states of his existence, irrespective of caste, creed, nationality, or boundary, the Vaiṣṇava is worshipable by all; therefore he is called *jagad-guru.* Those who are opposed to him are most abominable.

Our most worshipable beloved Swamin! You are *śikśā-guru, dīkṣa-guru* and *jagad-guru.* You are widely known as His Diving Grace A.C. Bhaktivedanta Swami Prabhupāda. We have come to know that you are the most munificent descendent of His Lordship Prabhupāda Śrīmad Bhaktisiddhanta Sarasvati Gosvami Thākura.
